功能模块说明戒指新增-获取戒指佩戴状态集成 ZH SDK
本文介绍 ZH SDK 的集成方式和具体步骤。 
前置条件
支持 Android5.0 及以上的版本。集成方式
 ZH SDK 提供"aar"文件的方式实现相关功能，需要把"aar"文件放置到 app 的 libs 目录下
配置 build.gradle
1.android.defaultConfig 
Kotlin
android {
   ...
    defaultConfig {
       ...
        ndk {
            abiFilters 'armeabi-v7a', 'arm64-v8a'
        }
    }
   ...
}
2.dependencies 
Kotlin
dependencies {
    //gson
    implementation 'com.google.code.gson:gson:2.13.1'
    //protobuf
    implementation 'com.google.protobuf:protobuf-java:4.34.1'
    //ZH SDK aar
    implementation files('libs/ZH_SDK_20XXXXXX_VX.X.X.aar')
}配置 AndroidManifest.xml
1.permission 
Kotlin
<uses-permission android:name="android.permission.ACCESS_FINE_LOCATION" />
<uses-permission android:name="android.permission.ACCESS_COARSE_LOCATION" />
<uses-permission android:name="android.permission.BLUETOOTH_SCAN" />
<uses-permission android:name="android.permission.BLUETOOTH_CONNECT" />
2.申请权限 
Java
if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.S){
    if (ActivityCompat.checkSelfPermission(this, Manifest.permission.BLUETOOTH_CONNECT) != PackageManager.PERMISSION_GRANTED) {
        ActivityCompat.requestPermissions(this, new String[]{
            Manifest.permission.BLUETOOTH_CONNECT,
            Manifest.permission.BLUETOOTH_SCAN
        }, 0x999);
        return;
    }
} else {
    if (ActivityCompat.checkSelfPermission(this, Manifest.permission.ACCESS_FINE_LOCATION) != PackageManager.PERMISSION_GRANTED) {
        ActivityCompat.requestPermissions(this, new String[]{
            Manifest.permission.ACCESS_FINE_LOCATION,
            Manifest.permission.ACCESS_COARSE_LOCATION
        }, 0x9999);
        return;
    }
}初始化 ZH SDK
Java
method
/**
 * 初始化SDK Initialize the SDK
 *
 * @param context 上下文 Context
 */
ControlBleTools.getInstance().init(Context context)；
Java
callBack
ControlBleTools.getInstance().setInitStatusCallBack(new ZHInitStatusCallBack() {
    @Override
    public void onInitComplete() {
        //...
    }
});释放 ZH SDK
Java
method
/**
 * 释放sdk Release SDK
 */
ControlBleTools.getInstance().release()；注册 SDK 日志回调
Java
method
/**
 * 注册 SDK 日志 Register SDK Log
 * @param callBack
 */
public void setDeviceLogCallBack(DeviceLogCallBack callBack)
Kotlin
example
ControlBleTools.getInstance().setDeviceLogCallBack(object : DeviceLogCallBack {
    override fun onLogI(mode: String?, tag: String?, msg: String?) {
        if (TextUtils.isEmpty(mode)) {
            Log.i(tag, msg!!)
            //TODO 将日志内容保存至本地文件 Save the log content to a local file
        } else {
            val str = java.lang.StringBuilder()
            str.append("<").append(mode).append(">").append("  ").append(msg)
            Log.i(tag, str.toString())
            //TODO 将日志内容保存至本地文件 Save the log content to a local file
        }
    }

    override fun onLogV(mode: String?, tag: String?, msg: String?) {
        ...
    }

    override fun onLogE(mode: String?, tag: String?, msg: String?) {
        ...
    }

    override fun onLogD(mode: String?, tag: String?, msg: String?) {
        ...
    }

    override fun onLogW(mode: String?, tag: String?, msg: String?) {
        ...
    }
})功能目录

Kotlin
example
var mLeScanCallback: LeScanCallback = LeScanCallback { device:BluetoothDevice, rssi:Int, scanRecord:ByteArray ->
      val scanResult = ScanResult(device, ScanRecord.parseFromBytes(scanRecord), rssi, SystemClock.elapsedRealtimeNanos())
      val scanDeviceBean = ScanDeviceBean(scanResult)
}
mBluetoothAdapter.startLeScan(mLeScanCallback);连接设备

SQL
bean
public class ScanDeviceBean implements Serializable {
    /**
     * 蓝牙设备 Bluetooth device
     */
    public BluetoothDevice device;
    /**
     * 协议代号 Protocol code
     */
    public String protocolName = "";
    /**
     * 协议版本号 Protocol version
     */
    public String protocolVersion = "";
    /**
     * rssi
     */
    public int rssi;
    /**
     * 昵称 Name
     */
    public String name = "";
    /**
     * mac地址 Mac address
     */
    public String address = "";

    /**
     * 是否被绑定 Is it bound
     */
    public boolean isBind = false;
    /**
     * 是否用户模式 Is it user mode
     */
    public boolean isUserMode = false;
    /**
     * 是否支持直连绑定，设备是否显示确认绑定界面 Does it support direct connection binding, and does the device display a confirmation binding interface
     */
    public boolean hasBindingFunction = false;
    /**
     * 是否支持BT通话蓝牙 Is it supported BT call bluetooth
     */
    public boolean isSupportHeadset = false;
    /**
     * BT蓝牙是否已配对 Is BT bluetooth already bonded
     */
    public boolean isHeadsetBond = false;
    /**
     * BT蓝牙是否已广播 Is BT bluetooth broadcast
     */
    public boolean isHeadsetBroadcast = false;
    /**
     * 是否处于思澈OTA模式 Is it in the state of Sifli OTA mode
     */
    public boolean isDfu = false;
    /**
     * BT蓝牙MAC  BT MAC
     */
    public String headsetMac = "";
    /**
     * 广播数据 Service data
     */
    public String serviceDataString;
    /**
     * 设备类型 Device type
     */
    public String deviceType;
    /**
     * 设备版本号 Device version
     */
    public int deviceVersion = 0;
    /**
     * 设备版本号 Device version name
     */
    public String deviceVersionName = "";

    /**
     * 是否支持一键双连 （仅Berry支持） Does it support one click dual connection (only supported by Berry)
     */
    public boolean isDualConnection = false;

    /**
     * 是否切换新手机模式 (仅Berry支持) Switch to new phone mode (only supported by Berry)
     */
    public boolean isNewPhoneMode = false;
}BleStateCallBack
Java
callback
public interface BleStateCallBack {
    /**
     * 连接状态回调 Connection status callback
     *
     * @param state 连接状态[0:断连,1:连接中,2:已连接,3:断连中,4:连接超时] Connection status [0: disconnected, 1: connecting, 2: connected, 3: disconnected, 4: connection timed out]
     * {@link BleCommonAttributes#STATE_DISCONNECTED,BleCommonAttributes#STATE_CONNECTING,BleCommonAttributes#STATE_CONNECTED,BleCommonAttributes#STATE_DISCONNECTING,BleCommonAttributes#STATE_TIME_OUT}
     */
    void onConnectState(int state);
}

example

}2. 功能集合

Java
callback
public interface VerifyUserIdCallBack {
    /**
     * 用户id是否一致 Is the user id consistent?
     *
     * @param state {@link VerifyUserIdState}
     */
    void onVerifyState(int state);

    public enum VerifyUserIdState {
        INCONSISTENT(0x01), //不一致 Inconsistency
        UNANIMOUS(0x00);    //一致 Consistency
    }
}RequestDeviceBindStateCallBack
Java
callback
public interface RequestDeviceBindStateCallBack {
    /**
     * 设备绑定状态回调 Device binding status callback
     * @param state 是否已绑定 Is it bound?
     */
    void onBindState(boolean state);
}BindDeviceStateCallBack
Java
callback
public interface BindDeviceStateCallBack {
    /**
     * 绑定设备回复状态信息回调 Bound device reply status information callback
     * @param bindDeviceBean
     */
    void onDeviceInfo(BindDeviceBean bindDeviceBean);

    public enum VerifyCode {
        /**
         * 绑定成功
         */
        SUCCESS(0x00),
        /**
         * 拒绝绑定(手动绑定)
         */
        REFUSE(0x01),
        /**
         * 超时(手动绑定)
         */
        OVER_TIME(0x02),
        /**
         * 校验失败(扫码绑定)
         */
        VERIFICATION_FAILED(0x03);
    }
}UnbindDeviceCallBack

public class BindDeviceBean implements Serializable {

     * 序列号 Serial number
     */
    public String serialNumber = "";
    /**
     *  设备号 Device number

    public String deviceNumber = "";
    /**

    public String firmwareVersion = "";

Java
callback
public interface BerryBindCallBack {
    /**
     * 发送用户id设备回复数据 Send user id device reply data
     *
     * @param bean
     */
    void onUserIdResult(BerryDeviceInfoBean bean);
    
    /**
     * 绑定结果回调 Binding result callback
     *
     * @param status {@link BindStatus}
     */
    void onBindStatus(int status);
  
    public enum BindStatus {
        /**
         * 绑定成功 Binding successful
         */
        SUCCESS(0x00),
        /**
         * 非同一用户ID Different user ID
         */
        DIFFERENT_USER_ID(0x01),
        /**
         * 用户点击拒绝 User clicks Reject
         */
        REFUSE(0x02),
        /**
         * 设备已绑定 Device bound
         */
        ALREADY_BOUND(0x03),
        /**
         * 设备已恢复出厂设置 The device has been restored to factory settings
         */
        ALREADY_RESET(0x04),
        /**
         * 超时 time out
         */
        OVER_TIME(0x05),
        /**
         * 解析失败 Parsing failed
         */
        PARSING_FAILED(0x06);
    }
    
    /**
     * 回复绑定成功结果回调 Reply to the binding success result callback
     *
     * @param status {@link BindSuccessStatus}
     */
    void onBindSuccess(int status);

    public enum BindSuccessStatus {
        /**
         * 绑定成功 Binding successful
         */
        SUCCESS(0x00),
        /**
         * 绑定失败 Binding failed
         */
        FAILED(0x01);
    }
  
}UnbindDeviceCallBack
Java
callback
public interface UnbindDeviceCallBack {
    /**
     * 解绑设备成功 Device unbinding successful
     */
    void unbindDeviceSuccess();
}BerryDeviceInfoBean
Java
bean
public class BerryDeviceInfoBean implements Serializable {
    /**
     * 设备型号 Device Model
     */
    private String deviceType;
    /**
     * 设备版本 Device version
     */
    private String deviceVersion;
    /**
     * 设备BLE MAC Device BLE MAC
     */
    private String deviceMac;
    /**
     * 设备通话蓝牙MAC Device call Bluetooth MAC
     */
    private String btMac;
    /**
     * 设备序列号 Device serial number
     */
    private String deviceSn;
    /**
     * 是否同一用户 Is it the same user?
     */
    private boolean isSameUser;
    /**
     * 是否绑定 Whether to bind
     */
    private boolean isBind;
}2.3 数据

Java
callback
public interface DeviceInfoCallBack {

    /**
     * 设备信息回调 Device information callback
     */
    void onDeviceInfo(DeviceInfoBean deviceInfoBean);

    /**
     * 电量信息回调 Battery information callback
     *
     * @param capacity     电量值 Battery value
     * @param chargeStatus 电量状态 Battery status {@link ChargeStatus}
     */
    void onBatteryInfo(int capacity, int chargeStatus);

    public enum ChargeStatus {
        UNKNOWN(0x00),      //未知状态 Unknown status
        CHARGING(0x01),     //充电中 Charging
        NOT_CHARGING(0x02), //没充电 Not charging
        FULL(0x03);         //充满状态 Full state
   }
}DeviceInfoBean
C++
bean
public class DeviceInfoBean implements Serializable {
    /**
     * 版本号 Version number
     */
    public String firmwareVersion = "";
    /**
     * 设备号 Device Number
     */
    public String equipmentNumber = "";
    /**
     * mac地址 MAC address
     */
    public String mac = "";
    /**
     * sn号 sn number
     */
    public String serialNumber = "";
    /**
     * 是否屏蔽设备遥控拍照开关信息(true表示去掉遥控拍照无功能，false表示有功能)(CY项目使用)
     * Whether to block the device remote control camera switch information
     * (true means to remove the remote control camera function, false means to enable the function)
     * (CY project use)
     */
    private boolean remoteCamera;
    /**
     * 设备运动带图标功能新协议协议(true表示新协议，false表示旧协议)(CY项目使用)
     * Device motion with icon function new protocol protocol
     * (true means new protocol, false means old protocol)
     * (CY project use)
     */
    private boolean isNewSportsIconProtocolSwitch;
    /**
     * 上次断连原因 Last disconnection reason
     */
    private long lastDisconnectReason;
    /**
     * 上次断连的时间戳 Timestamp of the last disconnection
     */
    private long lastDisconnectTimestamp;
    /**
     * 上次断连sdk内部记录日志 SDK internal log of the last disconnection
     */
    private String sdkLastDisconnectLog = "";
    /**
     * 是否支持Spp Whether to support Spp
     */
    public boolean sppSupportFlat = false;
}2.3.3 获取电量信息

Java
callback
public interface DeviceMptPowerLogCallBack {

    /**
     * 电量使用情况列表 Power usage list
     *
     * @param list
     */
    void onMptPowerLogList(List<MptPowerLogBean> list);

    /**
     * 耗电量类型枚举 Power consumption type enumeration
     */
    public enum PowerType {
        /**
         * 来电提醒 Caller Alert
         */
        CALLER_ALERT(0x01),
        /**
         * 信息提醒 SMS Alerts
         */
        SMS_ALERTS(0x02),
        /**
         * 手腕唤醒 Wrist wake
         */
        WRIST_WAKE(0x03),
        /**
         * 蓝牙通话 Bluetooth cal1
         */
        BLUETOOTH_CALL(0x04),
        /**
         * 闹钟 Alarm Clock
         */
        ALARM_CLOCK(0x05),
        /**
         * 呼吸锻炼 Breathing exercises
         */
        BREATHING_EXERCISES(0x06),
        /**
         * 压力 Stress levels
         */
        STRESS_LEVELS(0x07),
        /**
         * 血氧 Blood Oxygen
         */
        BLOOD_OXYGEN(0x08),
        /**
         * 心率 Heart rate
         */
        HEART_RATE(0x09),
        /**
         * 倒计时 Countdown
         */
        COUNTDOWN(0x0A),
        /**
         * 手电筒 Flashlight
         */
        FLASHLIGHT(0x0B),
        /**
         * 连续心率测试 Continuous heart rate
         */
        CONTINUOUS_HEART_RATE(0x0C);
    }
}MptPowerLogBean
Java
bean
public class MptPowerLogBean implements Serializable {
    /**
     * 日志产生开始时间戳 Log generation start timestamp
     */
    private int startTimestamp;

    /**
     * 日志产生结束时间戳 Log generation end timestamp
     */
    private int endTimestamp;

    /**
     * 当前剩余电量百分比 Current remaining battery percentage
     */
    private int powerPercent;

    /**
     * 耗电日志详情list Power consumption log details list
     */
    private List<PowerLog> powerLogs;

    public static class PowerLog implements Serializable {
        /**
         * 类型id Type id
         *
         * @see com.zhapp.ble.callback.DeviceMptPowerLogCallBack.PowerType
         */
        private int id;

        /**
         * 耗电触发次数 Power consumption trigger times
         */
        private int quantity;

        /**
         * 耗电百分比值 Power consumption percentage
         */
        private int percent;
    }

public class RealTimeBean extends BaseBean implements Serializable {

    public String bloodOxygen;

     */

     * 压力 Stress
     */

     * 当天当前的走路卡路里 (戒指项目这个总卡路里对标Oura的卡路里)
     * Current walking calories for the day (this total calories in the ring project is compared to Oura's calories)
     */
    public String todayOuraCalorie;
    /**
     * 走路卡路里数据 24小时数据  (戒指项目对标Oura的卡路里 24小时数据)
     * Walking calorie data 24 hours data (Ring project benchmark Oura 24 hours calorie data)
     */
    public List<Integer> todayOuraCalorieHourlyData;
    /**
     * 当天当前的运动卡路里
     * Current exercise calories for the day
     */
    public String todaySportCalorie;

     * 24-hour exercise calorie data (24-hour exercise calorie data for ring items)
     */
    public List<Integer> todaySportCalorieHourlyData;
    /**
     * 步数数据  24小时数据 Step data 24-hour data
     */
    public List<Integer> stepsData;
    /**
     * 距离数据  24小时数据 Distance data 24-hour data
     */
    public List<Integer> distanceData;
    /**
     * 卡路里数据  24小时数据  (戒指项目对标Apple的卡路里24小时数据)
     * Calorie data 24 hours data (the ring project is benchmarked against Apple's 24 hours calorie data)
     */
    public List<Integer> calorieData;

    /**
     * 最新喝水量 单位：ml Latest water intake Unit: ml
     */
    public int drinkWater;

    public RingChargingCaseInfoBean ringChargingCaseInfoBean;

    /**

     */
    public int ringSar;

     * Ring project NFC sleep error 0: indicates normal 1: indicates abnormal
     */
    public int nfcSleepErr;
    /**

     * Ring project charging completion time  Unit: minutes
     */
    public int fullyChargedNeedTime;
    /**
     * 戒指项目电池估算时间  单位：0.5天 (1代表0.5天 2代表一天)
     * Ring project remaining battery  Unit: 0.5 days (1 represents 0.5 days 2 represents 1 day)
     */
    public int remainingBatteryTime;

callback
public interface FitnessDataCallBack {

    /**
     * 进度 Progress
     *
     * @param progress 当前进度 Current progress
     * @param total    总进度  Overall progress
     */
    void onProgress(int progress, int total);

    /**
     * 日常数据 Daily data
     *
     * @param bean
     */
    void onDailyData(DailyBean bean);

    /**
     * 睡眠数据 Sleep data
     *
     * @param bean
     */
    void onSleepData(SleepBean bean);

    /**
     * 连续心率数据 Continuous heart rate data
     *
     * @param bean
     */
    void onContinuousHeartRateData(ContinuousHeartRateBean bean);

    /**
     * 离线心率数据 Offline heart rate data
     *
     * @param bean
     */
    void onOfflineHeartRateData(OfflineHeartRateBean bean);

    /**
     * 连续血氧数据 Continuous blood oxygen data
     *
     * @param bean
     */
    void onContinuousBloodOxygenData(ContinuousBloodOxygenBean bean);

    /**
     * 离线血氧数据 Offline blood oxygen data
     *
     * @param bean
     */
    void onOfflineBloodOxygenData(OfflineBloodOxygenBean bean);

    /**
     * 连续压力数据 Continuous pressure data
     * 戒指项目-该数据为HRV数据   Ring Project - This data is HRV data.
     *
     * @param bean
     */
    void onContinuousPressureData(ContinuousPressureBean bean);

    /**
     * 离线压力数据 Offline pressure data
     *
     * @param bean
     */
    void onOfflinePressureData(OfflinePressureDataBean bean);

    /**
     * 连续体温数据 Continuous temperature data
     *
     * @param bean
     */
    void onContinuousTemperatureData(ContinuousTemperatureBean bean);

    /**
     * 离线体温数据 Offline temperature data
     *
     * @param bean
     */
    void onOfflineTemperatureData(OfflineTemperatureDataBean bean);

    /**
     * 有效站立数据 Effective standing data
     *
     * @param bean
     */
    void onEffectiveStandingData(EffectiveStandingBean bean);

    /**
     * 活动时长数据 Activity duration data
     *
     * @param bean
     */
    void onActivityDurationData(ActivityDurationBean bean);

    /**
     * 离线ECG数据 Offline ECG data
     *
     * @param bean
     */
    void onOffEcgData(OffEcgDataBean bean);

    /**
     * 考试信息数据 Examination information data
     *
     * @param bean
     */
    void onExaminationData(ExaminationBean bean);

    /**
     * GoMore算法活动类型指标数据 GoMore algorithm activity type indicator data
     *
     * @param bean
     */
    void onRingTodayActiveTypeData(TodayActiveTypeData bean);

    /**
     * GoMore算法全天活动指标数据 GoMore algorithm all-day activity indicator data
     *
     * @param bean
     */
    void onRingOverallDayMovementData(OverallDayMovementData bean);

    /**
     * GoMore算法呼吸速率数据 GoMore algorithm respiratory rate data
     *
     * @param bean
     */
    void onRingTodayRespiratoryRateData(TodayRespiratoryRateData bean);

    /**
     * 戒指健康分数数据回调 Ring health score data callback
     *
     * @param bean
     */
    void onRingHealthScore(RingHealthScoreBean bean);

    /**
     * 获取戒指睡眠结果数据回调 Ring sleep result data callback
     *
     * @param bean
     */
    void onRingSleepResult(RingSleepResultBean bean);

    /**
     * 睡眠类型  Sleep type
     */
    public enum SleepDistributionType {
        /**
         * 清醒 Awake
         */
        AWAKE(0x00),
        /**
         * 浅睡 Light sleep
         */
        LIGHT_SLEEP(0x01),
        /**
         * 深睡 Deep sleep
         */
        DEEP_SLEEP(0x02),
        /**
         * 快速眼动 Rapid eye movement
         */
        RAPID_EYE_MOVEMENT(0x03);
    }

    /**
     * 睡眠活动的类型
     */
    public enum SleepMovementsType {
        /**
         * 无活动 No movement
         */
        NO_MOVEMENT(0x00),
        /**
         * 低 Low
         */
        LOW(0x01),
        /**
         * 中 Medium
         */
        MEDIUM(0x02),
        /**
         * 高 High
         */
        INTENSE(0x03);
    }

    /**
     * 戒指小睡数据 Ring small nap data
     *
     * @param list
     */
    void onRingSleepNAP(List<RingSleepNapBean> list);

    /**
     * 戒指多天的自动运动 Ring multi-day automatic motion
     *
     * @param bean
     */
    void onRingAutoActiveSportData(AutoActiveSportBean bean);

    /**
     * 戒指多天的自动运动的类型 Ring multi-day automatic motion type
     */
    public enum AutoActivesportType {
        /**
         * 未知 Unknown
         */
        UNDEFINED_TYPE(0x00),
        /**
         * 健走  Walk
         */
        WALK_TYPE(0x01),
        /**
         * 跑步  Run
         */
        RUN_TYPE(0x02);
    }

    /**
     * 戒指多天的自动运动的等级 Ring multi-day automatic motion level
     */
    public enum AutoActivesportLevel {
        /**
         * 低  Low
         */
        LOW(0x00),
        /**
         * 中  Medium
         */
        MEDIUM(0x01),
        /**
         * 高  High
         */
        HIGH(0x02);
    }

    /**
     * GoMore算法身体分数数据（戒指项目） GoMore algorithm body score data(Ring Project)
     *
     * @param bean
     */
    void onRingBodyBatteryData(RingBodyBatteryBean bean);

    /**

     * @param bean

    /**

     * @param bean
     */

    /**

     * @param bean
     */
    void onSleepRRIData(SleepRRIBean bean);

    /**

Java
bean
public class SleepBean extends BaseBean implements Serializable {
    /**
     * 总的睡眠起始时间：入睡时间 Total sleep onset time: time to sleep
     */
    public long startSleepTimestamp;
    /**
     * 总的睡眠结束时间：起床时间 Total sleep end time: wake-up time
     */
    public long endSleepTimestamp;
    /**
     * 总的睡眠持续时间：睡眠时长 Total sleep duration: sleep duration
     */
    public int sleepDuration;
    /**
     * 睡眠分数 Sleep Score
     */
    public int sleepScore;
    /**
     * 总的清醒时长 Total awake time
     */
    public int awakeTime;
    /**
     * 总的清醒时长百分比 Percentage of total awake time
     */
    public int awakeTimePercentage;
    /**
     * 总的浅睡时长 Total light sleep time
     */
    public int lightSleepTime;
    /**
     * 总的浅睡时长百分比 Percentage of total light sleep time
     */
    public int lightSleepTimePercentage;
    /**
     * 总的深睡时长 Total deep sleep time
     */
    public int deepSleepTime;
    /**
     * 总的深睡时长百分比 Percentage of total deep sleep time
     */
    public int deepSleepTimePercentage;
    /**
     * 总的快速眼动时长 Total rapid eye movement time
     */
    public int rapidEyeMovementTime;
    /**
     * 总的快速眼动时长百分比 Percentage of total rapid eye movement time
     */
    public int rapidEyeMovementTimePercentage;
    /**
     * 是否夜晚 Sleep at night
     */
    public boolean isNightSleep;
    /**
     * 睡眠分布 Sleep distribution
     */
    public List<SleepDistributionData> list = new ArrayList<>();
    /**
     * 睡眠准备得分 Sleep preparation score
     */
    public float readinessScore;

    public static class SleepDistributionData implements Serializable {
        /**
         * 睡眠的起始时间 Sleep onset time
         */
        public long startTimestamp;
        /**
         * 睡眠持续时间 Sleep duration
         */
        public int sleepDuration;
        /**
         * 睡眠分布类型 Sleep distribution type
         * {@link com.zhapp.ble.callback.FitnessDataCallBack.SleepDistributionType}
         */
        public int sleepDistributionType;
    }

Java
bean
public class ContinuousHeartRateBean extends BaseBean implements Serializable {
    /**
     * 连续心率频率 Continuous heart rate frequency
     */
    public int continuousHeartRateFrequency;
    /**
     * 频率版本 0:频率单位分钟 1:频率单位为秒 Frequency version 0: Frequency unit is minutes; 1: Frequency unit is seconds.
     */
    public int frequencyVersion;
    /**
     * 连续心率频率 Continuous heart rate
     *（0值代表无效值   A value of 0 represents an invalid value.）
     */
    public List<Integer> heartRateData;
    /**
     * 最大心率 Maximum heart rate
     */
    public int max;

public class RingSleepResultBean extends BaseBean implements Serializable {

     * 起床时间(单位:时间戳) Wake up time (unit: timestamp)
     */
    public int exitTime;
    /**
     * 睡眠时长(单位:秒) Sleep duration (unit: seconds)
     */
    public int sleepDuration;
    /**
     * 在床上的时间(单位:秒) Time in bed (in seconds)
     */
    public int timeInBedTime;
    /**
     * 尝试睡眠到睡着过程的总时间(单位:秒) The total time from trying to sleep to falling asleep (in seconds)
     */
    public int sleepLatency;
    /**
     * 睡眠效率 Sleep efficiency (0~100%)
     */
    public int sleepEfficiency;
    /**
     * 睡眠评分 0-29 睡眠很差 30-44 睡眠不佳  45-74 平均睡眠 75-99 良好睡眠
     * Sleep score 0-29 Very poor sleep 30-44 Poor sleep 45-74 Average sleep 75-99 Good sleep
     */
    public int sleepScore;

     * 总的清醒时长 Total awake time

    public int awakeTime;

    /**

     */
    public int deepSleepTime;
    /**
     * 总的快速眼动时长 Total rapid eye movement time
     */
    public int rapidEyeMovementTime;
    /**
     * 各个睡眠段落 Each sleep phase
     */
    public List<SleepDistribution> sleepDistributionData;
    /**
     * 各个活动睡眠段落
     */

         * 睡眠持续时间 Sleep duration
         */
        public int sleepDuration;

         * 睡眠活动的类型 Sleep activity type
         *
         * @see com.zhapp.ble.callback.FitnessDataCallBack.SleepDistributionType
         */
        public int sleepDistributionType;
    }

        /**
         * 睡眠活动的起始时间 Sleep movement start time

        public int startTimestamp;

Java
bean
public class DevSportInfoBean implements Cloneable, Serializable {
    /**
     * 定位数据 wgs84坐标系 格式：lon,lat;......
     * Positioning data WGS84 coordinate system format: lon, lat;......
     */
    private String map_data = "";
    /**
     * 原始数据的id，16进制字节码
     * The id of the original data, hexadecimal byte code
     */
    private String recordPointDataId = "";
    /**
     * 开始时间戳ms
     * Start time stamp ms
     */
    private long recordPointIdTime;
    /**
     * 时区
     * Time zone
     */
    private int recordPointTimeZone;
    /**
     * GPS版本
     * GPS version
     */
    private int recordGpsVersion;
    /**
     * 打点版本
     * Dot version
     */
    private int recordPointVersion;
    /**
     * 报告版本
     * Report version
     */
    private int recordReportVersion;
    /**
     * 描述0=保留(无效)，1=运动数据，默认1
     * Description 0=Invalid (invalid), 1=Sport data, default 1
     */
    private int recordPointTypeDescription;
    /**
     * 运动类型 Type of exercise
     * 1    户外跑步   Outdoor running

     * 26   拉伸 Stretching

    /**
     * 打点数据
     */
    public static class RecordPointSportData implements Cloneable, Serializable {
        /**
         * 海拔高度
         * Altitude
         */
        public float altitude;
        /**
         * 秒级时间戳 每10s一个点
         * Seconds timestamp every 10s
         */
        public long time;
        /**
         * 卡路里
         * Calorie
         */
        public int cal;
        /**
         * 步数
         * Step
         */
        public int step;
        /**
         * 心率 bmp
         * Heart rate
         */
        public int heart;
        /**
         * 是否整公里 满1公里变true之后又变false
         * Is it a whole kilometer? After reaching 1 kilometer, it changes to true and then changes to false.
         */
        public boolean isFullKilometer;
        /**
         * 升降类型 0=下降，1=上升
         * Lift type 0=Down, 1=Up
         */
        public int heightType;
        /**
         * 高度
         * Height
         */
        public double height;
        /**
         * 距离
         * Distance
         */
        public double distance;
        /**
         * 整英里
         * Is it whole mile？
         */
        public boolean isMile;

        /**
         * 整公里或整英里时间戳(秒)
         * Whole kilometer or whole mile timestamp (seconds)
         */
        public long timeMills;
        /**
         * 步幅(厘米)
         * Stride (cm)
         */
        public int stride;
        /**
         * 步频(步/分钟)
         * Cadence (steps/minute)
         */
        public int cadence;
        /**
         * 配速(秒/公里)
         * Pace (seconds/km)
         */
        public int pace;

        /**
         * 速度(公里/小时)
         * Speed (km/h)
         */
        public int speed;

        /**
         * 板球角色  数值0为击球手，数值1代表投球手，数值2代表接球手
         * Cricket Role Value 0 represents batsman, value 1 represents bowler, value 2 represents catcher
         */
        public int cricketRole;
    }

    public static class DeviceSportSwimEntity implements Cloneable, Serializable {
        /**
         * 数据类型 0）小节；1）段落
         * Data type 0）Section; 1）Paragraph
         */
        public int dataType;
        /**
         * 时间戳 ms
         * Timestamp ms
         */
        public long timeStamp;
        /**
         * 本小节主泳姿 0）混合泳；1）蛙泳；2）自由泳；3）仰泳；4）蝶泳
         * This section main swimming pose 0）Mixed swimming; 1）Frog swimming; 2）Free swimming; 3）Laying swimming; 4）But
         */
        public int style;
        /**
         * 本小节配速（秒/百米）
         * This section pace (seconds/100m)
         */
        public int pace;
        /**
         * 本小节swolf
         * This section swolf
         */
        public int swolf;
        /**
         * 累计里程（米）
         * Accumulated mileage (meters)
         */
        public int distance;
        /**
         * 累计卡路里（千卡）
         * Accumulated calorie (kilojoules)
         */
        public int cal;
        /**
         * 累计划水次数（次）
         * Accumulated plan water times (times)
         */
        public int frequency;
        /**
         * 累计转身次数（次）
         * Accumulated turn times (times)
         */
        public int turns;
        /**
         * 本小节划频（次/分钟）
         * This section swipe (times/minute)
         */
        public int swipe;
        /**
         * 本小节未知泳姿划水次数（次）
         * This section unknown swimming stroke times (times)
         */
        public int strokes0;
        /**
         * 本小节蛙泳划水次数（次）
         * This section frog swimming stroke times (times)
         */
        public int strokes1;
        /**
         * 本小节自由泳划水次数（次）
         * This section free swimming stroke times (times)
         */
        public int strokes2;
        /**
         * 本小节仰泳划水次数（次）
         * This section lay swimming stroke times (times)
         */
        public int strokes3;
        /**
         * 本小节蝶泳划水次数（次）
         * This section butterfly swimming stroke times (times)
         */
        public int strokes4;
    }

    public static class RingPointData implements Cloneable, Serializable {
        /**
         * 打点时间戳
         * Point time stamp
         */
        public long pointTime;
        /**
         * 运动强度
         * Exercise intensity
         */
        public int exerciseIntensity;
        /**
         * 心率置信度
         * Heart rate confidence
         */
        public int heartRateConfidence;
        /**
         * 心率
         * Heart rate
         */
        public int heartRate;
    }
}

public interface FirmwareLogStateCallBack {
    /**
     * 设备日志文件传输进度状态 Device log file transfer progress status
     *
     * @param state {@link FirmwareLogState}
     */
    void onFirmwareLogState(int state);

    /**
     * File path after device log transfer is completed
     * @param filePath 日志文件路径 Log file path
     */
    void onFirmwareLogFilePath(String filePath);

    /**
     * 日志文件传输进度状态枚举
     */
    enum FirmwareLogState {
        START(0x00),       //开始上传 Start uploading
        UPLOADING(0x01),   //正在上传 Uploading
        END(0x02);         //结束数据上传 End data upload
    }
}FirmwareTrackingLogCallBack

Java
callback
public interface DeviceLargeFileStatusListener {
    /**
     * 大文件状态回调 Large file status
     * @param statusValue 状态 Status {@link DeviceLargeFileStatusListener.PrepareStatus}
     * @param statusName  状态名称 Status name
     */
    void onSuccess(int statusValue, String statusName);

    /**
     * 超时 Time out
     */
    void timeOut();

    public enum PrepareStatus {
        READY(0x00),                        //准备好了  Ready
        BUSY(0x01),                         //忙碌 Busy
        DUPLICATED(0x02),                   //重复 Duplicate
        LOW_STORAGE(0x03),                  //内存不足 Out of memory
        LOW_BATTERY(0x04),                  //电量不足 Low battery
        DOWNGRADE(0x05),                    //降级，强制 Downgrade, forced
        LOW_STORAGE_TO_ASK_DELETE(0x06),    //内存不足询问是否删除表盘（只针对表盘） Insufficient memory asks whether to delete the watch face (only for watch faces)
        NEW_ERROR(0x07),                    //设备类型错误 Device type error
        DEVICE_ID_ERROR(0x08),              //设备ID异常 Device ID abnormality
        OVER_SPACE(0x09),                   //文件大小超出设备存储空间 The file size exceeds the device storage space
        PATRIARCH_INFO_ID_ERROR(0x0A),      //家长信息id错误 Parent information ID is incorrect
        FLASH_CARD_COUNT_ERROR(0x0B);       //闪存卡数量异常 Abnormal number of flash memory cards
    }
}UploadBigDataListener
Java
callback
public interface UploadBigDataListener {
    /**
     * 成功 Success
     */
    void onSuccess();

    /**
     * 传输进度 Transmission progress
     *
     * @param curPiece                 当前进度 Current progress
     * @param dataPackTotalPieceLength 总进度 Current progress
     */
    void onProgress(int curPiece, int dataPackTotalPieceLength);

    /**
     * 异常或超时 error or timeout
     *
     * @param msg 信息  Message
     */
    void onTimeout(String msg);
}2.4.2 AGPS

Java

public class AgpsInfoBean implements Serializable {
    /**
     * 是否需要更新AGPS  need to update AGPS
     */
    private boolean isNeed;
    /**
     * 类型
     * AGPS = 0x01;
     * BEIDOU = 0x02;
     * GALILEO = 0x03;
     */
    private int type;
    /**
     * 源
     * source must be consistent with server
     * eg: ublox/broadcom/airoha
     */
    private String source;
    /**
     * 过期天数 Expired days
     */
    private int days;
    /**
     * 一些 GPS 供应商需要 GPS 信息，例如 airoha
     * some gps vendor need gps info, such as airoha
     */
    private boolean needGpsInfo;
    /**
     * 分片大小，单位为字节，默认4096 agps data slice
     */
    private int expectedSliceLength;
}获取文件状态

WatchFaceListBean
C++
callback
public class WatchFaceListBean implements Serializable {
    /**
     * 表盘id Dial id
     */
    private String id;
    /**
     * 是否当前 Is it current
     */
    private boolean isCurrent;
    /**
     * 是否移除 Is it removed
     */
    private boolean isRemove;
    /**
     * 表盘名称  Name
     */
    private String name;
    /**
     * 样式  Style
     */
    private String style;
    /**
     * 相册表盘支持的图片格式  Supported image format
     * @see com.zhapp.ble.callback.WatchFaceListCallBack.ImageFormat
     */
    private int supportImageFormat;
    /**
     * 表盘相册背景md5  Dial album background md5
     */
    private String backgroundImage;

    /**
     * 表盘相册背景md5集合  Dial album background md5 list
     *
     */
    private List<String> backgroundImageList;

   /**
    * 表盘版本  Version
    * 0/1：仅支持单个背景 背景md5取值 backgroundImage   0/1: Supports only a single background, using the background MD5 hash of `backgroundImage`.
    * 2支持2-10个背景 背景md5取值 backgroundImageList  2: Supports 2-10 backgrounds, using the background MD5 hash of `backgroundImageList`.
    */
    private long versionCode;
}2.6.2 普通表盘
获取发送表盘状态
Java
method
/**
 * 获取发送表盘文件状态

}DeviceWatchFaceFileStatusListener
Java
bean
public interface DeviceWatchFaceFileStatusListener {
    /**
     * 表盘状态 Watch Face Status
     *
     * @param statusValue {@link DeviceWatchFaceFileStatusListener.PrepareStatus}
     * @param statusName  状态名称
     */
    void onSuccess(int statusValue, String statusName);

    /**
     * 超时 time out
     */
    void timeOut();

    public enum PrepareStatus {
        READY(0x00),       //准备好了 Ready
        BUSY(0x01),        //忙碌 Busy
        DUPLICATED(0x02),  //重复  Duplicated
        LOW_STORAGE(0x03), //内存不足 Out of memory
        LOW_BATTERY(0x04), //电量不足 Low battery
        DOWNGRADE(0x05),   //降级，强制升级 Downgrade, forced upgrade
        LOW_STORAGE_TO_ASK_DELETE(0x06); //内存不足询问是否删除表盘（只针对表盘） Insufficient memory asks whether to delete the watch face (only for watch faces)
    }
}WatchFaceInstallCallBack
Java
callback
public interface WatchFaceInstallCallBack {
    /**
     * 表盘安装结果 Dial installation results
     *
     * @param result
     */
    void onresult(WatchFaceInstallResultBean result);

    public enum WatchFaceInstallCode {
        VERIFY_FAILED(0x00),    //验证失败 Verification failed
        INSTALL_FAILED(0x01),   //安装失败 Install failed
        INSTALL_SUCCESS(0x02),  //安装成功 Install success
        INSTALL_USED(0x03);     //安装失败已使用 Install failed used
    }
}WatchFaceInstallResultBean

bean
public class NewZhDiyDialBean implements Serializable {
    /**
     * 表盘id  Dial id
     */
    private String id;
    /**
     * 表盘版本  Dial version
     */
    private int watchVersion;
    /**
     * 表盘形状  Dial shape
     */
    private String shape;
    /**
     * 表盘分辨率  Dial resolution
     */
    private String dpi;
    /**
     * 表盘缩略图分辨率  Dial thumbnail resolution
     */
    private String thumbnailDpi;
    /**
     * 表盘缩略图偏移量  Dial thumbnail offset
     */
    private String thumbnailOffset;
    /**
     * 表盘类型  Dial type
     */
    private String type;
    /**
     * 表盘作者  Dial author
     */
    private String author;
    /**
     * 表盘编号  Dial code
     */
    private String code;
    /**
     * 表盘名称  Dial name
     */
    private String watchName;
    /**
     * 表盘描述  Dial description
     */
    private String watchDesc;
    /**
     * 表盘背景  Dial background
     */
    private BackgroundBean background;
    /**
     * 表盘复杂功能  Watch face complications
     */
    private ComplexBean complex;
    /**
     * 覆盖图路径  Overlay image path
     */
    private String[] overlayImgPaths;
    /**
     * 表盘缩略图  Dial thumbnail
     */
    private List<ScaleImage> scaleImages;
    /**
     * 表盘指针  Dial pointers
     */
    private List<PointersBean> pointers;
    /**
     * 表盘数字  Dial numbers
     */
    private List<NumbersBean> time;

    public static class ScaleImage implements Serializable{
        /**
         * 设计稿图片路径  Design sketch image path
         */
        private String designsketchImgPath;
        /**
         * 覆盖图图片路径  Overlay image path
         */
        private String overlayImgPath;
    }

    public static class BackgroundBean implements Serializable {
        /**
         * 表盘背景图片的md5值  Dial background image md5
         */
        private String md5Background;
        /**
         * 表盘背景图片路径  Dial background image path
         */
        private String backgroundImgPath;
        /**
         * 表盘背景图片缩略图的md5值  Dial background image thumbnail md5
         */
        private String md5Thumbnail;
        /**
         * 表盘背景图片缩略图路径  Dial background image thumbnail path
         */
        private String thumbnailImgPath;
        /**
         * 设计草图图路径  Design sketch path
         */
        private String designsketchImgPath;
    }

    public static class ComplexBean implements Serializable {
        /**
         * 是否压缩  Is compressed
         */
        private boolean isCompress;
        /**
         * md5值  md5
         */
        private String md5;
        /**
         * 路径  path
         */
        private String path;
        /**
         * 信息集合  Info list
         */
        private List<InfosBean> infos;

        public static class InfosBean implements Serializable {
            /**
             * location : MidUp
             * detail : [{"isDefault":true,"typeName":"Kwh","pointX":152,"pointY":92,"picPath":"/complex/Kwh.png"},{"isDefault":false,"typeName":"GeneralDate","pointX":152,"pointY":92,"picPath":"/complex/GeneralDate.png"},{"isDefault":false,"typeName":"Step","pointX":152,"pointY":92,"picPath":"/complex/Step.png"},{"isDefault":false,"typeName":"Calorie","pointX":152,"pointY":92,"picPath":"/complex/Calorie.png"}]
             */

            /**
             * 位置  Location
             */
            private String location;
            /**
             * 详情集合  Details list
             */
            private List<DetailBean> detail;

            public static class DetailBean implements Serializable {
                /**
                 * 是否默认  Is default
                 */
                private boolean isDefault;
                /**
                 * 类型名称  Type name
                 */
                private String typeName;
                /**
                 * 点X坐标  Point X
                 */
                private int pointX;
                /**
                 * 点Y坐标  Point Y
                 */
                private int pointY;
                /**
                 * 图片路径  Pic path
                 */
                private String picPath;
            }
        }
    }

    public static class PointersBean implements Serializable {
        /**
         * 是否压缩  Is compress
         */
        private boolean isCompress;
        /**
         * md5值  Md5
         */
        private String md5;
        /**
         * 指针图片路径  Pointer img path
         */
        private String pointerImgPath;
        /**
         * 指针数据路径  Pointer data path
         */
        private String pointerDataPath;
        /**
         * 指针叠加图片路径  Pointer overlay path
         */
        private String pointerOverlayPath;
    }

    public static class NumbersBean implements Serializable {
        /**
         * 字体昵称 Fonts name
         */
        private String fontsName;
        /**
         * 是否压缩  Is compress
         */
        private boolean isCompress;
        /**
         * 位置信息  Location info
         */
        private List<LocationInfo> locationInfos;
        /**
         * md5值  Md5
         */
        private String md5;
        /**
         * 时间数据路径  Time data path
         */
        private String timeDataPath;

        public static class LocationInfo implements Serializable {
            /**
             * 是否默认  Is default
             */
            private boolean isDefault;
            /**
             * 位置名称 Location name
             */
            private String locationName;
            /**
             * 文本信息  Text info
             */
            private List<TextInfo> textInfos;
            /**
             * 时间图片路径  Time img path
             */
            private String timeImgPath;
            /**
             * 时间叠加图片路径  Time overlay path
             */
            private String timeOverlayPath;

            public static class TextInfo implements Serializable {
                /**
                 * 字体颜色  Fonts color
                 */
                private int colorAddr;
                /**
                 * 左边地址 Left addr
                 */
                private int leftAddr;
                /**
                 * 左边值 Left value
                 */
                private int leftValue;
                /**
                 * 上边地址 Top addr
                 */
                private int topAddr;
                /**
                 * 上边值 Top value
                 */
                private int topValue;
                /**
                 * 是否禁用 Is disable
                 */
                private int isDisableAddr;
                /**
                 * 是否禁用 Is disable value
                 */
                private int isDisableValue;
            }
        }
    }

bean
public class DiyWatchFaceConfigBean implements Cloneable, Serializable {
    /**
     * 表盘ID Dial id
     */
    private String id;
    /**
     * 规则 Rule
     */
    private int ruleCount;
    /**
     * 复杂功能 Complications
     */
    private List<FunctionsConfig> functionsConfigs;
    /**
     * 复杂功能文件 Complex Functions File
     */
    private WatchFaceFileConfig functionsFileConfig;
    /**
     * 指针Bin文件 Pointer bin
     */
    private WatchFaceFileConfig pointerFileConfig;
    /**
     * 数字Bin文件 Number bin
     */
    private WatchFaceFileConfig numberFileConfig;
    /**
     * 预览图（缩略图）文件 Preview(Thumbnail) File
     */
    private WatchFaceFileConfig previewFileConfig;
    /**
     * 背景图文件 Background File
     */
    private WatchFaceFileConfig backgroundFileConfig;

    /**
     * 是否全文件数据  Whether the full file data
     */
    private boolean isAllFiles = false;

    /**
     * 是否不需要文件 No file required
     */
    private boolean isNotNeedFile = false;

    /**
     * 复杂功能配置类
     */
    public static class FunctionsConfig implements Cloneable, Serializable {
        /**
         * 复杂功能位置 Complication Location
         *
         * @see com.zhapp.ble.callback.DiyWatchFaceCallBack.DiyWatchFaceLocation
         */
        private int position;
        /**
         * 复杂功能 Complications
         *
         * @see com.zhapp.ble.callback.DiyWatchFaceCallBack.DiyWatchFaceFunction
         */
        private int typeChoose;
        /**
         * 复杂功能类型 Complication Type
         */
        private List<FunctionsConfigType> functionsConfigTypes;
    }

    /**
     * 文件资源配置类
     */
    public static class WatchFaceFileConfig implements Cloneable, Serializable {
        /**
         * 这个类型的个数 The number of this type
         */
        private int watchFaceNumber;
        /**
         * 正在使用的文件编号 The file number in use
         */
        private int usedFileNumber;
        /**
         * 文件集合 File Collection
         */
        private List<WatchFaceFile> watchFaceFiles;

        public static class WatchFaceFile implements Cloneable, Serializable {
            /**
             * 文件大小 File Size
             */
            private int fileSize;
            /**
             * 文件编号 背景 1-50  缩略图 51-100 复杂功能 101-110 指针 111-120 数字121-130
             * File Number Background 1-50 Thumbnail 51-100 Complication 101-110 Hands 111-120 Numbers 121-130
             */
            private int fileNumber;
            /**
             * 文件MD5 File MD5
             */
            private String fileMd5;
            //以下为可选项 The following are optional
            /**
             * 背景覆盖图md5 Background overlay md5
             */
            private String backgroundOverlayMd5;
            /**
             * 数字字体md5 Number Font md5
             */
            private String numberFontMd5;
            /**
             * 数字位置md5 Number Location md5
             */
            private String numberLocationMd5;
            /**
             * 数字颜色md5 Number Color md5
             */
            private int numberColorHex;

            /**
             * 背景颜色MD5 Background Color MD5
             */
            private int backgroundColorHex;
        }
    }

C++
callback
public interface DiyWatchFaceCallBack {
    /**
     * diy表盘配置
     *
     * @param config
     */
    void onDiyWatchFaceStatus(DiyWatchFaceConfigBean config);


    /**
     * 表盘位置枚举
     */
    public enum DiyWatchFaceLocation {
        /**
         * 左上 left up
         */
        LEFT_UP(0x01),
        /**
         * 中上 middle up
         */
        MIDDLE_UP(0x02),
        /**
         * 右上 right up
         */
        RIGHT_UP(0x03),
        /**
         * 左中 left middle
         */
        LEFT_MIDDLE(0x04),
        /**
         * 中 middle
         */
        MIDDLE(0x05),
        /**
         * 右中 right middle
         */
        RIGHT_MIDDLE(0x06),
        /**
         * 左下 left down
         */
        LEFT_DOWN(0x07),
        /**
         * 中下 middle down
         */
        MIDDLE_DOWN(0x08),
        /**
         * 右下下 right_down
         */
        RIGHT_DOWN(0x09);
    }

    /**
     * 表盘复杂功能类型
     */
    public enum DiyWatchFaceFunction {
        /**
         * 关闭 Off
         */
        OFF(0x00),
        /**
         * 电量 battery
         */
        BATTERY(0x01),
        /**
         * 日期 date
         */
        GENERAL_DATE(0x02),
        /**
         * 步数 step
         */
        STEP(0x03),
        /**
         * 卡路里 calorie
         */
        CALORIE(0x04),
        /**
         * 心率 heart rate
         */
        HEART_RATE(0x05);
    }

public class OldDiyParamsBean implements Serializable {

     * 指针 或者 数字 资源 pointer or number resources
     */
    private StyleResBean styleResBean;
    /**
     * 复杂功能  Complications
     */
    private FunctionsResBean functionsResBean;

    /**
     * 复杂功能设置 diy watch face config

    public static class BackgroundResBean {
        /**
         * 背景图资源 background resource
         */
        private Bitmap background;

        /**
         * 背景覆盖图资源 background overlay resource
         */
        private Bitmap backgroundOverlay;
    }


    public static class StyleResBean {

        /**
         * 类型 Type
         * @see StyleResBean.StyleType
         */
        private int type;

        /**
         * 指针图资源 pointer resource
         */
        private Bitmap styleBm;

        /**
         * 背景图资源 background resource
         */
        private byte[] styleBin;

        public enum StyleType {
            /**
             * 指针 pointer
             */
            POINTER(0x01),
            /**
             * 数字 number
             */
            NUMBER(0x02);
        }
    }


    /**
     * 功能资源类
     */
    public static class FunctionsResBean {
        /**
         * 复杂功能bin资源 functions bin resource
         */
        private byte[] functionsBin;

        /**
         * 复杂功能type图片资源 functions type resource
         */
        private List<FunctionsBitmapBean> functionsBitmaps;

        public static class FunctionsBitmapBean {
            /**
             * 功能图资源 functions bitmap resource
             */
            private Bitmap bitmap;

            /**
             * 功能名称 functions name
             * @see com.zhapp.ble.callback.DiyWatchFaceCallBack.DiyWatchFaceFunction
             */
            private int function;
        }
    }

Java
callback
public interface BerryWatchFaceStatusCallBack {

    /**
     * 表盘状态回复 Watch face status reply
     *
     * @param bean
     */
    void onPrepareStatus(BerryWatchFaceStatusReplyBean bean);

    public enum PrepareStatus {
        READY(0x00),       //准备好了  Ready
        BUSY(0x01),        //忙碌  Busy
        DUPLICATED(0x02),  //重复 Duplicated
        LOW_STORAGE(0x03), //内存不足 Low storage
        LOW_BATTERY(0x04), //电量不足 Low battery
        DOWNGRADE(0x05),   //降级，强制升级 Downgrade
        LOW_STORAGE_TO_ASK_DELETE(0x06); //内存不足询问是否删除表盘（只针对表盘） Low storage to ask delete
    }
}BerryDialUploadListener 
Java
callback
public interface BerryDialUploadListener {
    /**
     * 成功 Success
     *
     * @see SucCode
     */
    void onSuccess(int code);

    /**
     * 传输进度  Transfer progress
     *
     * @param curPiece                 当前进度 Current progress
     * @param dataPackTotalPieceLength 总进度 Current progress
     */
    void onProgress(int curPiece, int dataPackTotalPieceLength);

    /**
     * 异常或超时  Exception or timeout
     *
     * @param msg 信息  Message
     */
    void onTimeout(String msg);

    public enum SucCode {
        SUCCESS(0x00), //表盘，背景传输成功 Dial, background transfer successful
        SUCCESS_BUT_LOW_STORAGE(0x01), //no storage for all background image, but other optional valid, as success
        FAIL_FOR_LOW_STORAGE(0x02), // no storage for any background image, as fail directly
        FAIL_FOR_INVALID_PARAM(0x03), // background color or style invalid
        BACKGROUND_FAIL(0x04),  // fail for any other reason
        IMAGE_SAVE_FAILED(0x05), //image save failed for any reason
        IMAGE_RESOLVE_FAILED(0x06), //image resolve failed, such as format not support...
        NO_WATCH_FACE_MATCHED(0x07); // can not find corresponding watch face
    }
}BerryAlbumWatchFaceEditRequestBean 
Java
bean
public class BerryAlbumWatchFaceEditRequestBean implements Serializable {
    /**
     * 表盘id Dial ID
     */
    private String id;
    /**
     * 设置当前 Set current
     */
    private boolean setCurrent = true;
    /**
     * 样式 style
     */
    private String style;
    /**
     * 背景id image id: md5, must match mass data id
     */
    private String backgroundImageMD5;
    /**
     * 背景大小
     */
    private int backgroundImageSize;

    /**
     * 背景ids image ids: md5, must match mass data id
     */
    private List<String> backgroundImageMD5s;

    /**
     * 背景大小集合 Background size collection
     */
    private List<Integer> backgroundImageSizes;

    /**
     * 图片切换样式 0：每次点亮切换； 1：30分钟切换
     * Image switching style 0: Switch each time it is lit; 1: Switch every 30 minutes
     */
    private int imageSwitchStyle;
}流程图

public class WeatherPerHourBean implements Serializable {

     */
    public int minute;
    /**
     * 秒  Second
     */
    public int second;

     * 城市名称 CityName
     */
    public String cityName;

     */
    public String locationName;

     * 数据  Data
     */

        /**
         * 当前温度（摄氏度） Current temperature (Celsius)
         */

         * 最低温（摄氏度） Low temperature (Celsius)
         */
        public int low_temperature;

        public int high_temperature;

        public int humidity;

         */
        public String weather_name;

        public int Wind_speed;

         * 风向 Wind direction
         */
        public int wind_info;

        public int Probability_of_rainfall;
        /**
         * 紫外线 UVI

        public int ultraviolet_rays;

public class BerryLatestWeatherBean implements Serializable {
    /**
     * 天气信息 Weather info
     */
    private BerryWeatherIdBean id;
    /**
     * 天气ID Weather ID  https://openweathermap.org/weather-conditions#Weather-Condition-Codes
     */
    private int weather;
    /**
     * 气温 Temperature
     */
    private BerryWeatherKeyValueBean temperature;
    /**
     * 湿度 Humidity %
     */
    private BerryWeatherKeyValueBean humidity;
    /**
     * 风力等级 Wind force level 0-12
     */
    private BerryWeatherKeyValueBean windSpeed;
    /**
     * 风向 wind direction 0-360
     */
    private BerryWeatherKeyValueBean windDeg;
    /**
     * 防晒指数 紫外线强度 Sun Protection Factor UV Intensity
     */
    private BerryWeatherKeyValueBean uvindex;
    /**
     * 空气质量 优 aqi >=0 && aqi<= 50 良 aqi >50 && aqi<= 100 轻度污染 aqi >100
     * Air quality Excellent aqi >=0 && aqi<= 50 Good aqi >50 && aqi<= 100 Slightly polluted aqi >100
     */
    private BerryWeatherKeyValueBean aqi;
    /**
     * 预警信息 Alerts
     */
    private List<WeatherAlertsListBean> alertsList;
    /**
     *  大气压强 Pressure
     */
    private float pressure;

    public static class WeatherAlertsListBean {
        /**
         * 预警id Warning ID
         */
        private String id;
        /**
         * 预警类型 String 例："大风"
         * Warning type String Example: "strong wind"
         */
        private String type;
        /**
         * 预警级别 String 例："蓝色"
         * Warning level String Example: "blue"
         */
        private String level;
        /**
         * 预警标题 String 例："本溪市大风蓝色预警"
         * Warning title String Example: "Beijing Strong Wind Blue Warning"
         */
        private String title;
        /**
         * 预警详情 String 例："本溪市大风蓝色预警本溪市大风蓝色预警本溪市大风蓝色预警本溪市大风蓝色预警"
         * Warning details String Example: "Beijing Strong Wind Blue Warning Beijing Strong Wind Blue Warning Beijing Strong Wind Blue Warning Beijing Strong Wind Blue Warning"
         */
        private String detail;
    }

TypeScript
bean
public class BerryForecastWeatherBean implements Serializable {
    /**
     * 天气信息 Weather info
     */
    private BerryWeatherIdBean id;
    /**
     * 天气数据 Weather data
     */
    public List<WeatherData> data;

    public static class WeatherData implements Serializable{
        /**
         * 空气质量 优 aqi >=0 && aqi<= 50 良 aqi >50 && aqi<= 100 轻度污染 aqi >100
         *Air quality Excellent aqi >=0 && aqi<= 50 Good aqi >50 && aqi<= 100 Slightly polluted aqi >100
         */
        private BerryWeatherKeyValueBean api;
        /**
         * 开始天气id - 结束天气id
         * Weather id from - Weather id to
         */
        private BerryWeatherRangeValueBean weather;
        /**
         * 最低气温 - 最高气温
         * Temperature from - Temperature to
         */
        private BerryWeatherRangeValueBean temperature;
        /**
         * 温度单位
         * Temperature unit
         */
        private String temperatureUnit;
        /**
         * 日出日落 秒级时间戳
         * Sunrise and sunset timestamps in seconds
         */
        private BerryWeatherSunRiseSetBean sunRiseSet;
        /**
         * 风力等级 0-12
         * Wind strength level 0-12
         */
        private BerryWeatherKeyValueBean windSpeed;
        /**
         * 风向 0-360
         * Wind direction 0-360
         */
        private BerryWeatherKeyValueBean windDeg;
    }

    public static class BerryWeatherRangeValueBean{
        /**
         * 范围开始值
         * Range start value
         */
        private int from;
        /**
         * 范围结束值
         * Range end value
         */
        private int to;
    }

    public static class BerryWeatherSunRiseSetBean{
        /**
         * 日出时间 秒级时间戳
         * Sunrise time in seconds
         */
        private long sunRise;
        /**
         * 日落时间 秒级时间戳
         * Sunset time in seconds
         */
        private long sunSet;
    }
}BerryWeatherKeyValueBean

public interface SecondaryScreenSportCallBack {
    /**
     * 设备运动状态 The device motion status
     * 
     * @param bean 
     */
    void onSportStatus(SportStatusBean bean);
    /**
     * 设备回复app副屏运动状态上报 The device replies to the app's secondary screen motion status report
     *
     * @param bean
     */
    void onSecondaryScreenSportResponseBean(SportResponseBean bean);
    
    /**
     * 设备上报副屏运动状态 The device reports the motion status of the secondary screen
     *
     * @param bean
     */
    void onSecondaryScreenSportRequestBean(SportRequestBean bean);
    
    /**
     * 副屏运动过程中数据 The device reports the data of the motion status of the secondary screen
     * @param bean
     */
    void onSecondaryScreenWearData(SecondaryScreenWearDataBean bean);

    public enum SportLevel {
        /**
         * 未活动 Inactive
         */
        INACTIVE(0),
        /**
         * 低 Low
         */
        LOW(1),
        /**
         * 中 Medium
         */
        MEDIUM(2),
        /**
         * 高  High
         */
        HIGH(3);
    }
}SportResponseBean
C++
bean
public class SportResponseBean implements Serializable {
    //辅助运动 app --> 设备  SPORT_REQUEST_VALUE
    //副屏运动 设备 --> APP
    /**
     * 状态回应 0 OK; 1 设备正忙; 2 恢复/暂停类型不匹配; 3 没有位置权限; 4 运动不支持; 5 精确gps关闭或后台无gps许可; 6 充电中; 7 低电量 ; 8 定位失败； 9 运动前空间将满--当不能储存运动三小时的数据或运动记录条数大于15条；10 未知； 11 运动前空间已满--运动记录条数大于20后flash存储空间不能继续存储 ； 12 设备处于运动中
     * Status response 0 OK; 1 Device is busy; 2 Resume/pause type does not match; 3 No location permission; 4 Sports not supported; 5 Precise GPS is off or there is no background GPS permission;
     * 6 Charging; 7 Low battery; 8 Positioning failed; 9 Pre-exercise space is almost full - when it cannot store three hours of exercise data or the number of exercise records is greater than 15; 10 Unknown; 11 Pre-exercise space is full - when the number of exercise records exceeds 20, the flash storage space cannot continue to store; 12 Device is in motion
     */
    public int code;
    /**
     * GPS状态 低 0; 中 1; 高 2; 未知 10;
     * GPS accuracy low 0; medium 1; high 2; unknown 10;
     */
    public int gpsAccuracy;
    /**
     * 运动状态 Movement status
     */
    public SportStatusBean sportStatus;
    /**
     * 运动支持的版本 (副屏运动 未启用) Versions supported by motion (secondary screen motion is not enabled)
     */
    public int selectVersion;
}SportStatusBean

public interface WearActiveMeasureCallBack {
    /**
     * 设备发送主动测量 成功或失败回调 The device sends an active measurement success or failure callback
     *
     * @param statusBean
     */
    void onMeasureStatus(ActiveMeasureStatusBean statusBean);

    /**
     * 设备发送主动测量 测量中的数据回调 The device sends active measurement data callback
     *
     * @param measuringBean
     */
    void onMeasuring(ActiveMeasuringBean measuringBean);

    /**
     * 设备发送主动测量 测量结果数据回调 The device sends active measurement result data callback
     *
     * @param resultBean
     */
    void onMeasureResult(ActiveMeasureResultBean resultBean);


    /**
     * 测量类型枚举
     */
    public enum MeasureType {
        /**
         * 心率 heart rate
         */
        HEART_RATE(0x01),
        /**
         * 血氧 blood oxygen
         */
        BLOOD_OXYGEN(0x02),
        /**
         * 压力 stress
         */
        STRESS(0x03),
        /**
         * 体温 body temperature
         */
        BODY_TEMPERATURE(0x04);
    }

    /**
     * 测量类型枚举
     */
    public enum MeasureErrorReason {
        /**
         * 成功了的  succeeded
         */
        SUCCEEDED(0x00),
        /**
         * 未佩戴 not wearing
         */
        NOT_WRIST(0x01),
        /**
         * 数据异常 data error
         */
        DATA_ERROR(0x02);
    }
}ActiveMeasureParamsBean

bean

Java
callback
public interface SettingMenuCallBack {
    /**
     * EV电动车数据 EV electric vehicle data
     *
     * @param evDataInfoBean
     */
    void onEvDataInfo(EvDataInfoBean evDataInfoBean);

    /**
     * EV电动车连接状态
     */
    public enum EvConnectType {
        /**
         * 未连接 Not connected
         */
        EV_APP_CONNECT_NULL(0x00),
        /**
         * 获取中 Obtaining
         */
        EV_APP_CONNECT_ACQUIRE(0x01),
        /**
         * 获取成功  Get Success
         */
        EV_APP_CONNECT_SUCCEDD(0x02),
        /**
         * 获取超时 Get Timeout
         */
        EV_APP_CONNECT_OUT(0x03),
        /**
         * 获取失败/错误 Get Failure/Error
         */
        EV_APP_CONNECT_FALL(0x04);
    }

    /**
     * EV数据获取失败原因
     */
    public enum DataFailType {
        /**
         * 失败原因未知 Fail Reason Unknown
         */
        EV_APP_CONNECT_ERROR_NULL(0x00),
        /**
         * 用户尚未在 TVS Connect 上登录设备 The user has not logged into the device on TVS Connect
         */
        EV_APP_CONNECT_NOT_LOG_IN(0x01),
        /**
         * 用户已从 TVS Connect 上退出 The user has logged out of the device on TVS Connect
         */
        EV_APP_CONNECT_LOG_OUT(0x02),
        /**
         * NFA Watch 已从配件中移除 NFA Watch has been removed from the accessory
         */
        EV_APP_CONNECT_NFA_WATCH_REMOVE(0x03),
        /**
         * 离线观看 - 车辆与互联网失去连接 Offline Watch - Vehicle and Internet Connection Lost
         */
        EV_APP_CONNECT_OFF_LINE(0x04),
        /**
         * 没有从移动应用程序收到 VIN 和/或令牌新手入门 No VIN and/or token onboarding
         */
        EV_APP_CONNECT_NO_VIN(0x05),
        /**
         * 用户没有必要的订阅权限 User does not have necessary subscription permission
         */
        EV_APP_CONNECT_NO_PERMISSION(0x06),
        /**
         * P360 API 导致 400 错误 P360 API caused 400 error
         */
        EV_APP_CONNECT_API_400_ERROR(0x07),
        /**
         * 用户已加入 -- 确保设备已连接到 NoiseFit 应用并可访问互联网 User Joined -- Make sure the device is connected to the NoiseFit app and has internet access
         */
        EV_APP_CONNECT_USER_IS_ONBOARDED(0x08),
        /**
         * 异常，无法获取数据 Exception, unable to get data
         */
        EV_APP_CONNECT_ERROR_ERROR(0XFF);
    }


    /**
     * EV电动车状态
     */
    public enum EvCarStatus {
        /**
         * 未知 Unknown
         */
        EV_CAR_NULL(0x00),
        /**
         * 锁定  Lock
         */
        EV_CAR_LOCKING(0x01),
        /**
         * 解锁 Unlock
         */
        EV_CAR_UNLOCK(0x02),
        /**
         * 正在骑行 Cycling
         */
        EV_CAR_CYCLING(0x03),
        /**
         * 异常，无法获取数据 Exception, unable to get data
         */
        EV_CAR_ERROR(0xFF);
    }

    /**
     * 车辆充电情况
     */
    public enum EvChargeStatus {
        /**
         * 未知 Unknown
         */
        EV_CHARGE_NULL(0x00),
        /**
         * 未充电 Not Charging
         */
        EV_CHARGE_NO(0x01),
        /**
         * 充电中 Charging
         */
        EV_CHARGE_ING(0x02),
        /**
         * 充满  Full
         */
        EV_CHARGE_FULL(0x03),
        /**
         * 异常，无法获取数据 Exception, unable to get data
         */
        EV_CHARGE_END(0xFF);
    }
}SettingMenuCallBack.onEvRemindType

        EV_TRALIER_STEAL_REMIND(0x02),
        /**
         * 位置提醒 Location Reminder
         */
        EV_LOCATION_REMIND(0x03),
        /**
         * 碰撞/坠落警报提醒 Collision/fall alarm reminder
         */
        EV_CRASH_FALL_REMIND(0x04),
        /**
         * 异常，无法获取数据 Exception, unable to get data
         */
        EV_APP_CONNECT_ERROR(0xFF);
    }

TypeScript
bean
public class EvDataInfoBean implements Serializable {

    /**
     * 连接状态 Connection Status
     *
     * @see SettingMenuCallBack.EvConnectType
     */
    private int dataConnectType;

    /**
     * App数据 App data
     */
    private ApplicationData applicationData;

    /**
     * 获取失败原因 Failure reason for acquisition
     *
     * @see SettingMenuCallBack.DataFailType
     */
    private int dataFailType;

    /**
     * 刷新间隔 刷新频率（目前由设备强制10分钟） Refresh Interval Refresh frequency (currently forced by the device to 10 minutes)
     */
    private int refreshInterval;

    public static class ApplicationData {
        /**
         * 时间 Time
         */
        private TimeBean time;

        /**
         * 车辆状态 Vehicle status
         *
         * @see SettingMenuCallBack.EvCarStatus
         */
        private int carStatus;

        /**
         * 充电状态 Charge status
         */
        private ChargeStatus chargeStatus;

        /**
         * 剩余里程 米 Remaining mileage meters
         */
        private RemainingMile remainingMile;

        /**
         * 胎压 Tire pressure
         */
        private TireGauge tireGauge;

        /**
         * 显示  Display
         */
        private Display display;

        public static class ChargeStatus {
            /**
             * 充电状态 Charging status
             *
             * @see SettingMenuCallBack.EvChargeStatus
             */
            private int status;

            /**
             * 当前电量 Current power
             */
            private int currentCharge;

            /**
             * 充满电时间 分钟 Full charge time minute
             */
            private int fullChargeTime;
        }

        public static class RemainingMile {
            /**
             * 运动模式剩余里程 Remaining mileage in sports mode
             */
            private int sportMode;

            /**
             * 动力模式剩余里程 Remaining mileage in power mode
             */
            private int powerMode;

            /**
             * 节能模式剩余里程 Remaining mileage in eco mode
             */
            private int ecoMode;
        }

        public static class TireGauge {
            /**
             * 前轮胎压 无数据填 65535 Front tire pressure No data to fill 65535
             */
            private int frontWheel;

            /**
             * 后轮胎压 无数据填 65535 Rear tire pressure No data to fill 65535
             */
            private int rearWheel;
        }

        public static class Display {
            /**
             * 行驶总距离 米 Total distance travelled (m)
             */
            private int totalDistance;
            /**
             * 总碳排放量 千克kg Total carbon emissions kg
             */
            private double totalCarbonEmission;
            /**
             * 当天行驶的总距离 米 Cur day total distance (m)
             */
            private int curDayTotalDistance;
            /**
             * 当天平均速度 米/秒 Cur day avg speed (m/s)
             */
            private double curDayAvgSpeed;
            /**
             * 当天行驶的总时长 分钟 Cur day total driving time (min)
             */
            private int curDayTotalDrivingTime;
        }
    }

public class BreathingLightSettingsBean implements Serializable {

    /**
     * 呼吸灯总开关 Breathing light master switch
     */
    public boolean mainSwitch;
    /**
     * 呼吸灯常亮开关 Breathing light constant on switch
     */
    public boolean lighttingSwitch;
    /**
     * 呼吸灯常量颜色 argb Breathing light constant color argb
     */
    public int lighttingColor;
    /**
     * 呼吸灯列表 Breathing light list
     */
    public List<LightItem> lightItems;

    public static class LightItem implements Serializable {

        /**
         * 呼吸灯类型 Breathing light type
         * @see com.zhapp.ble.callback.SettingMenuCallBack.BreathingLightType
         */
        public int lightType;
        /**
         * 呼吸灯开关 Breathing light switch
         */
        public boolean lightSwitch;
        /**
         * 呼吸灯颜色 argb Breathing light color argb
         */
        public int lightColor;
    }

public interface AiFunctionCallBack {
    /**
     * 设备AI语音指令 Device AI voice commands
     *
     * @param bean
     */
    void onDevAiVoiceCmd(AiVoiceCmdBean bean);

    /**
     * 设备AI语音数据 Device AI voice data
     *
     * @param data
     */
    void onDevAiVoiceData(byte[] data);

    /**
     * 录音状态 Recording status
     */
    public enum AiVoiceCmd {
        /**
         * 无意义 Meaningless
         */
        CMD_NONE(0x00),
        /**
         * 开始(询问) Start (ask)
         */
        CMD_START(0x01),
        /**
         * 结束 End
         */
        CMD_END(0x02),
        /**
         * 可传输 Start transmission
         */
        CMD_TRANS(0x03);
    }

    /**
     * 错误码 Error code
     */
    public enum AiErrorCode {
        /**
         * 正常  OK
         */
        OK(0x00),
        /**
         * 网络异常 Network exception
         */
        NETWORK_ERROR(1),
        /**
         * 无语音超时 No voice timeout
         */
        NO_VOICE_TIMEOUT(2),
        /**
         * ASR / 理解失败 ASR / understanding failure
         */
        ASR_ERROR(3),
        /**
         * 服务器无响应 Server no response
         */
        SERVER_NO_RESPONSE(4),
        /**
         * 超过速率限制 Too many requests
         */
        RATE_LIMIT_ERROR(5);
    }

    /**

    public enum AiActionScenario {

        SET_HIGH_STRESS_ALERT(3),

         */
        SET_SLEEP_TARGET(4),
        /**
         * 设置步数目标 Set a step count goal
         */
        SET_STEPS_GOAL(5),
        /**
         * 设置卡路里目标 Set a calorie goal
         */
        SET_DISTANCE_GOAL(6),
        /**
         * 设定活动小时目标 Set an active hour goal
         */
        SET_ACTIVE_HOURS_GOAL(7),
        /**
         * 设定卡路里目标 Set a calorie goal
         */
        SET_CALORIES_GOAL(8),
        /**

         */
        SET_STANDING_GOAL(9),

        LOG_LAST_PERIOD_DATE(10),

        LOG_PERIOD_LENGTH(11),

public class AiHistoryUiBean implements Serializable {
    /**
     * 标题，例如：Heart Rate History  Title, for example: Heart Rate History
     */
    private String title;
    /**
     * 时期，例如：Last 7 Days  Period, for example: Last 7 Days
     */
    private String period;
    /**
     * 右边值显示(7天平均睡眠，平均心率等)  The value on the right (7-day average sleep, average heart rate, etc.)
     */

    /**
     * Y坐标最大值，例如：120 Maximum Y coordinate value, for example: 120
     */
    private int yMax;
    /**
     * 摘要数据 Summary Data
     */
    private Summary summary;
    /**
     * 表格数据列表 Table data list
     */
    private List<ChartData> chartData;

    public static class Summary implements Serializable {
        /**
         * 最大值 Maximum
         */
        private int maxValue;
        /**
         * 最小值 Minimum
         */
        private int minValue;
        /**
         * 平均值  Average
         */
        private int avgValue;
        /**
         * 单位  Unit
         */
        private String unit;
    }

         */

        /**
         * 月  Month
         */
        private int month;
        /**
         * 日  Day
         */
        private int day;

         * 星期，周日至周六：0，1，2，3，4，5，6 Day of the week, Sunday to Saturday: 0, 1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6
         */
        private int week;
        /**
         * 绘表值 Chart value
         */
        private int chartValue;
        /**
         * 类别 （Normal，Elevated，High...） Category (Normal, Elevated, High...)
         */
        private String category;
    }

callbback
public interface WearSocketCallBack {
    /**
     * socket配置信息 socket configuration information
     *
     * @param config
     */
    void onSocketConfig(WearSocketConfig config);

    /**
     * 设备发送Socket数据 The device sends socket data
     *
     * @param data
     */
    void onWearSendSocketMessage(WearSocketMessageData data);

    /**
     * 设备请求获取Socket数据 The device requests to obtain socket data
     *
     * @param data
     */
    void onWearRequestSocketMessage(WearSocketMessageData data);

    /**
     * 设备Socket断开连接 The device socket is disconnected.
     */
    void onSocketDisconnect(WearSocketResp resp);

    /**
     * 设备请求网络状态回调 Device request network status callback
     */
    void onRequestNetworkStatus();
}WearSocketConfig
C++
bean
public class WearSocketConfig implements Serializable {
    /**
     * IP地址或者域名 IP address or domain name
     */
    private String host;
    /**
     * 通道类型：1-TCP，2-UDP Channel type: 1-TCP, 2-UDP
     */
    private int type;
    /**
     * 端口号  Port
     */
    private int port;
    /**
     * 连接超时时间，单位：ms Connect timeout, unit: ms
     */
    private int connectTimeout;
    /**
     * 发送数据超时时间，单位：ms Send data timeout, unit: ms
     */
    private int sendTimeout;
    /**
     * 接收数据超时时间，单位：ms Receive data timeout, unit: ms
     */
    private int recvTimeout;
}WearSocketMessageData
Java
bean
public class WearSocketMessageData implements Serializable {
    /**
     * Socket通道建立成功返回的唯一ID标识 The unique ID returned when the socket channel is successfully established
     */
    private int uuid;
    /**
     * 传输的数据 The transmitted data
     */
    private byte[] messageData;
}WearSocketResp

bean
public class WearSocketResp implements Serializable {
    /**
     * Socket通道建立成功返回的唯一ID标识 The unique ID returned when the socket channel is successfully established
     */
    private int uuid;
    /**
     * 类型回复：1-成功 2-网络异常 3-超时失败等 The type of reply: 1-success 2-network exception 3-timeout failure etc
     */
    private int respType;

public interface MicroCallBack {
    /**
     * 设备端找手机 Find your phone on the device
     *
     * @param mode {@link FindPhoneMode}
     */
    void onWearSendFindPhone(int mode);

    public enum FindPhoneMode {
        /**

        /**

        FIND_STOP(0x01);
    }

public interface MicroCallBack {

     * 设备触发摇摇拍照

     * @param mode {@link PhotographMode} 0 开始摇一摇拍照 1 结束摇一摇拍  2 拍照

    public enum PhotographMode {
        /**
         * 进入摇一摇拍照 Enter Shake to take a photo

        /**
         * 结束摇一摇拍照 End Shake to take photo
         */
        PHOTOGRAPH_STOP(0x01),
        /**
         * 拍照 Photograph

        PHOTOGRAPHING(0x02);
    }

        ALBANIAN(0x01), //阿尔巴尼亚语

C++
bean
public class UserInfo implements Serializable {
    /**
     * 昵称 90字节30中文字符 Nickname (90 bytes 30 Chinese characters)
     */
    public String userName = "";
    /**
     * 身高 cm Height (cm)
     */
    public int height = -1;
    /**
     * 体重 kg Weight (kg)
     */
    public float weight = -1;
    /**
     * 生日  Birthday
     */
    public int birthday = -1;
    /**
     * 性别（1：男 2：女） Gender (1: Male 2: Female)
     */
    public int sex = -1;
    /**
     * 年龄 Age
     */
    public int age = -1;
    /**
     * 最大心率（次/分） Maximum heart rate (beats/min)
     */
    public int maxHr = -1;
    /**
     * 卡路里目标 千卡 Calorie goal (kcal)
     */
    public int calGoal = -1;
    /**
     * 步数目标 步 Step goal (steps)
     */
    public int stepGoal = -1;
    /**
     * 距离目标 米 Distance goal (m)
     */
    public int distanceGoal = -1;
    /**
     * 有效站立目标 次 Valid standing goal (times)
     */
    public int standingTimesGoal = -1;
    /**
     * 睡眠目标 分钟 Sleep goal (minutes)
     */
    public int goalSleepMinute = -1;
    /**
     * 活动时长目标 分钟 （传音项目增加） Activity duration goal (minutes)
     */
    public int activityDurationGoal = -1;
    /**
     * 喝水目标 ml Water drinking goal (ml)
     */
    public int goalDrinkWater = -1;
    /**
     * 步数开关 （传音项目增加）Step switch
     */
    public Boolean stepGoalOnOff;
    /**
     * 卡路里开关 （传音项目增加） Calorie switch
     */
    public Boolean calGoalOnOff;
    /**
     * 活动时长开关 （传音项目增加） Activity duration switch
     */
    public Boolean activityDurationOnOff;
    /**
     * 天气开关  Weather switch
     */
    public Boolean appWeatherSwitch;
    /**
     * 用户睡眠开始时间 Sleep start time
     */
    public SettingTimeBean userSleepStartTime;
    /**
     * 用户睡眠结束时间 Sleep end time
     */
    public SettingTimeBean userSleepEndTime;
}3.2 提醒设置

/**
 * 发送系统通知 Send system notification
 *
 * @param type         0来电 1未接来电 2短信 0 incoming calls 1 missed call 2 text messages
 * @param key          聚合通知消息唯一值 Unique value of aggregate notification message
 * @param sysName      系统应用名 System application name
 * @param sysPageName  系统应用包名 System application package name
 * @param phoneNumber  手机号 Phone number
 * @param contactsInfo 联系人昵称 Contact Nickname
 * @param messageText  （短信提醒）：内容 正文200个中文字符串长度，最大600个字节 (SMS reminder): Content: 200 Chinese characters in length, maximum 600 bytes
 */
public void sendSystemNotification(int type, String key, String sysName, String sysPageName, String phoneNumber, String contactsInfo, String messageText, ParsingStateManager.SendCmdStateListener listener)

}CallStateCallBack
Java
callBack
public interface CallStateCallBack {
    /**
     * 来电状态改变 Incoming call status changes
     * @param state {@link CallState}
     */
    void onState(int state);

    public enum CallState {
        /**
         * 接电话 Answering the phone
         */
        ANSWER_PHONE(0x00),
        /**
         * 挂电话 Hang up the phone
         */
        HANG_PHONE(0x01),
        /**
         * 静音 Mute
         */
        MUTE(0x02);
    }
}3.2.2 第三方通知
