method
/**
 * 发送第三方应用通知 Send third-party app notifications

 * @param appName    App名 App Name
 * @param pageName   App包名 App package name

 * @param text       通知内容 200个中文字符串长度，最大600个字节 Notification content: 200 Chinese characters in length, maximum 600 bytes

public interface SettingMenuCallBack {
    ...
    /**
     * 通知设置
     *
     * @param settingsBean
     */
    void onNotificationSetting(NotificationSettingsBean settingsBean);

    public enum NotificationSettingsVibrationMode {
        /**
         * 无震动 No vibration
         */
        NO_VIBRATION(0x00),
        /**
         * 震动等级1 Vibration level 1
         */
        VIBRATION_1(0x00),
        /**
         * 震动等级2 Vibration level 2
         */
        VIBRATION_2(0x01),
        /**
         * 震动等级3 Vibration level 3
         */
        VIBRATION_3(0x02);
    }

    public enum NotificationSettingsRingMode {
        /**
         * 无响铃 No ring
         */
        NO_RING(0x00),
        /**
         * 响铃1 Ring  1
         */
        RING_1(0x00);
    }
    ...
}NotificationSettingsBean
Java
bean
public class NotificationSettingsBean implements Serializable {
    /**
     * 通知不亮屏 Notifications don't light up the screen
     */
    public boolean noticeNotLightUp;
    /**
     * 来电提醒震动模式 Incoming call reminder vibration mode
     *
     * @see com.zhapp.ble.callback.SettingMenuCallBack.NotificationSettingsRingMode
     */
    public int phoneRemindVibrationMode;
    /**
     * 来电提醒响铃模式 Incoming call reminder ring mode
     *
     * @see com.zhapp.ble.callback.SettingMenuCallBack.NotificationSettingsVibrationMode
     */
    public int phoneRemindRingMode;
    /**
     * 延时提醒开关 Delay reminder switch
     */
    public boolean delayReminderSwitch;

    /**
     * 仅手机锁屏时通知 Only phone lock screen
     */
    public boolean isOnlyLockedNotify;
    /**
     * 仅佩戴通知 Only worn
     */
    public boolean isOnlyWornNotify;

public class ClockInfoBean implements Serializable {
    /**
     * ID
     */
    public int id = -1;
    /**
     * 数据 Data
     */
    public DataBean data;

    public static class DataBean implements Serializable {
        /**
         * 时间  Time
         */
        public SettingTimeBean time;
        /**
         * 闹钟的星期表示，1bit位表示一个星期  The week of the alarm clock is indicated by 1 bit, representing one week.
         */
        public int weekDays;
        /**
         * 是否开启状态 Enable status
         */
        public boolean isEnable;
        /**
         * 闹钟名字  Name
         */
        public String clockName;
        /**
         * 闹钟标签 Clock label
         */
        public int clockLabel;
        /**
         * 星期一是否选中 Monday is selected
         */
        public boolean isMonday;
        /**
         * 星期二是否选中 Tuesday is selected
         */
        public boolean isTuesday;
        /**
         * 星期三是否选中 Wednesday is selected
         */
        public boolean isWednesday;
        /**
         * 星期四是否选中 Thursday is selected
         */
        public boolean isThursday;
        /**
         * 星期五是否选中 Friday is selected
         */
        public boolean isFriday;
        /**
         * 星期六是否选中 Saturday is selected
         */
        public boolean isSaturday;
        /**
         * 星期日是否选中 Sunday is selected
         */
        public boolean isSunday;
    }3.2.13 久坐提醒

Java
bean
public class PhysiologicalCycleBean implements Serializable {
    /**
     * 提醒开关 Reminder switch
     */
    public boolean remindSwitch;
    /**
     * 是否已经设置过了 Has it been set up?
     */
    public boolean preset;
    /**
     * 提前多少天提醒 Reminder before how many days
     */
    public int advanceDay;
    /**
     * 总周期长度 Total cycle length
     */
    public int totalCycleDay;
    /**
     * 生理期长度 Cycle length
     */
    public int physiologicalCycleDay;
    /**
     * 经期开始日期 Start date of menstruation
     */
    public DateBean physiologicalStartDate;
    /**
     * 生理周期开关 Physiological cycle switch
     */
    public boolean physiologicalCycleSwitch;

    public static class DateBean implements Serializable {
        /**
         * 年 Year
         */
        public int year;
        /**
         * 月 Month
         */
        public int month;
        /**
         * 日  Day
         */
        public int day;
    }
}3.2.19 睡眠提醒

Java
callback
public interface DeviceReminderEventCallBack {
    /**
     * 设备提醒事件 Device Alert Events
     * @param event {@link DeviceReminderEvent}
     */
    void onDeviceReminderEvent(int event);
    

    public enum DeviceReminderEvent {
        /**
         * 未知 Unknown
         */
        UNKNOWN(0x00),
        /**
         * 高心率 High heart rate
         */
        HIGH_HEART_RATE(0x01),
        /**
         * 低心率 Low heart rate
         */
        LOW_HEART_RATE(0x02),
        /**
         * 低血氧 Low blood oxygen
         */
        LOW_BLOOD_OXYGEN(0x03),
        /**
         * 高HRV High HRV
         */
        HIGH_HRV(0x04),
        /**
         * 呼吸放松 Breathing relaxation
         */
        BREATHING_RELAXATION(0x05),
        /**
         * 久坐 Long sitting
         */
        LONG_SITTING(0x06);
    }
}

Java
bean
public class SuperNotificationBean implements Serializable {
    public static final int NOTIFICATION_TYPE_TAXI = 0;
    public static final int NOTIFICATION_TYPE_TAKEOUT = 1;
    public static final int NOTIFICATION_TYPE_PARCEL = 2;
    public static final int NOTIFICATION_TYPE_OTT = 3;
    public static final int NOTIFICATION_TYPE_CALENDAR = 4;
    public static final int NOTIFICATION_TYPE_HEALTH = 5;
    public static final int NOTIFICATION_TYPE_MESSAGING = 6;
    public static final int NOTIFICATION_TYPE_COMMUNICATION = 7;
    public static final int NOTIFICATION_TYPE_FINANCE = 8;
    public static final int NOTIFICATION_TYPE_DATING = 9;

    /**
     * 通知类型 Notification Type
     */
    public int notificationType;
    /**
     * 应用名称 App Name
     */
    public String appName;
    /**

}MusicCallBack 
Java
callback
public interface MusicCallBack {
    /**
     * 设备请求APP下发音乐信息 The device requests the APP to send music information
     */
    void onRequestMusic();

    /**
     * APP发送指令状态回调 APP sends command status callback
     *
     * @param state 状态码 State Code {@link com.zhapp.ble.parsing.SendCmdState}
     */
    void onSyncMusic(int state);

    /**
     * 设备退出音乐 Device exits music
     */
    void onQuitMusic();

    /**
     * 设备发送相关音乐指令 The device sends relevant music instructions
     *
     * @param command 音乐控制指令 Music control commands {@link MusicCommand}
     */
    void onSendMusicCmd(int command);

    public enum MusicCommand {
        PLAYING(0x01),              //改变播放状态(播放) Change playback status (play)
        PAUSE(0x02),                //改变播放状态(暂停) Change playback status (pause)
        PREV(0x03),                 //上一曲 Previous song
        NEXT(0x04),                 //下一曲 Next song
        ADJUST_VOLUME_UP(0x05),     //改变音量-加 Change volume-add
        ADJUST_VOLUME_DOWN(0x06);   //改变音量-减 Change volume-subtract
    }
}MusicInfoBean
Java
bean
public class MusicInfoBean implements Serializable {
    /**
     * 状态[0=无歌曲播放,1=无权限,2=播放中,3=已暂停 ,4=已停止,5=未播放音乐] 
     * State[0=No music playing,1=No permission,2=Playing,3=Paused,4=Stopped,5=No music playing]
     */
    public int state;
    /**
     * 标题  Title
     */
    public String songTitle;
    /**
     * 当前音量 Current volume
     */
    public int currentVolume;
    /**
     * 总音量 Total volume
     */
    public int totalVolume;
    /**
     * 是否是新文案 Is it a new copy
     */
    public boolean isNewPermissionType;
}3.4.3 股票

public interface SettingMenuCallBack {
    /**
     * 连续心率检测数据 Continuous heart rate monitoring data
     *
     * @param bean
     */
    void onHeartRateMonitorResult(HeartRateMonitorBean bean);
    
    public enum HeartRateMode {
        /**
         * 开启 Open
         */
        AUTO(0x00), 
        /**
         * 关闭 Close
         */
        OFF(0x01);
    }
    
    public enum ContinuousHeartRateMode {
        /**
         * 全天心率 All day heart rate
         */
        ALL_DAY_HEART_RATE(0x00), 
        /**
         * 智能心率 Intelligent heart rate
         */
        INTELLIGENT_HEART_RATE(0x01);
    }
}HeartRateMonitorBean
Java
bean
/**
 * 连续心率检测数据
 */
public class HeartRateMonitorBean implements Serializable {
    /**
     * 模式 Mode
     *
     * @see com.zhapp.ble.callback.SettingMenuCallBack.HeartRateMode
     */
    public int mode;
    /**
     * 测量频率(多少分钟检测一次，该参数仅支持APP获取，不支持设置)
     * Measurement frequency (how many minutes the test is performed, this parameter can only be obtained by the APP and cannot be set)
     */
    public int frequency;
    /**
     * 心率预警开关 Heart rate warning switch
     */
    public boolean isWarning;
    
    /**
     * 心率预警值 Heart rate warning value
     */
    public int warningValue;
    /**
     * 运动心率预警开关 Exercise heart rate warning switch
     */
    public boolean isSportWarning;
    /**
     * 运动心率预警值 Exercise heart rate warning value
     */
    public int sportWarningValue;
    /**
     * 连续心率模式 Continuous heart rate mode
     *
     * @see com.zhapp.ble.callback.SettingMenuCallBack.ContinuousHeartRateMode
     */
    public int continuousHeartRateMode;

    /**
     * 心率过低预警值 Low heart rate warning value
     */
    public int lowWarningValue;
}3.4.5 实时心率检测（zyb 定制）

public class SchedulerBean implements Serializable {
    /**
     * alert支持的最大数目 The maximum number of alerts supported
     */
    public int alertMaxSize;
    /**
     * habit支持的最大数目 The maximum number of habits supported
     */
    public int habitMaxSize;
    /**
     * remind支持的最大数目 The maximum number of reminders supported
     */
    public int reminderMaxSize;
    /**
     * habit时间设置支持的最大数目 The maximum number of habit time settings supported
     */
    public int supportMaxHabitTimeSet;
    /**
     * 警报列表 The alert list
     */
    public ArrayList<AlertBean> alertList;
    /**
     * 习惯列表 The habit list
     */
    public ArrayList<HabitBean> habitBeanList;
    /**
     * 提醒列表 The reminder list
     */
    public ArrayList<ReminderBean> reminderBeanList;

    public static class AlertBean implements Serializable {
        /**
         * id
         * */
        public int alertBerryId;
        /**
         * 名称 Name
         * */
        public String alertName;
        /**
         * 推送内容 Notify text
         * */
        public String notifyText;
        /**
         * 推送时间 Alert time
         * */
        public SettingTimeBean alertTime;
        /**
         * 星期表示，1bit位表示一个星期
         * Week indication, 1 bit represents a week
         * */
        public int weekDays;
        /**
         * 星期一是否选中 Is Monday selected?
         * */
        public boolean isMonday;
        /**
         * 星期二是否选中 Is Tuesday selected?
         * */
        public boolean isTuesday;
        /**
         * 星期三是否选中 Is Wednesday selected?
         * */
        public boolean isWednesday;
        /**
         * 星期四是否选中 Is Thursday selected?
         * */
        public boolean isThursday;
        /**
         * 星期五是否选中 Is Friday selected?
         * */
        public boolean isFriday;
        /**
         * 星期六是否选中 Is Saturday selected?
         * */
        public boolean isSaturday;
        /**
         * 星期日是否选中 Is Sunday selected?
         * */
        public boolean isSunday;
    }


    public static class HabitBean implements Serializable {

        public static int HABIT_CUSTOM_TYPE = SettingMenuProtos.SEHabitType.CUSTOM_TYPE_VALUE;              //自定义

        public static int HABIT_BRUSH_TEETH_TYPE = SettingMenuProtos.SEHabitType.BRUSH_TEETH_TYPE_VALUE;    //刷牙
        public static int HABIT_GET_UP_TYPE = SettingMenuProtos.SEHabitType.GET_UP_TYPE_VALUE;              //起床
        public static int HABIT_MEDITATION_TYPE = SettingMenuProtos.SEHabitType.MEDITATION_TYPE_VALUE;      //冥想
        public static int HABIT_STUDY_TYPE = SettingMenuProtos.SEHabitType.STUDY_TYPE_VALUE;                //学习
        public static int HABIT_WASH_HANDS_TYPE = SettingMenuProtos.SEHabitType.WASH_HANDS_VALUE;           //洗手
        public static int HABIT_READ_TIME_TYPE = SettingMenuProtos.SEHabitType.READING_TIME_VALUE;          //阅读
        public static int HABIT_TOILET_TIME_TYPE = SettingMenuProtos.SEHabitType.TOILET_TIME_VALUE;         //如厕
        /**
         * id
         * */
        public int habitBerryId;
        /**
         * 类型 Type
         * */
        public int habitType;
        /**
         * 名称  Name
         * */
        public String habitName;
        /**
         * 时间  Time
         * */
        public ArrayList<SettingTimeBean> habitTimeList;
        /**
         * 星期表示，1bit位表示一个星期
         * Week indication, 1 bit represents a week
         * */
        public int weekDays;
        /**
         * 星期一是否选中 Is Monday selected?
         * */
        public boolean isMonday;
        /**

bean
public class SchedulerResultBean implements Serializable {

    /**
     * 调度器ID  scheduler ID
     */
    private int id;
    /**
     * 结果 result x 0  √  1
     */
    private boolean result;
    /**
     * 调度器类型  scheduler type
     * ALERT = 0x00;
     * HABIT = 0x01;
     * REMINDER = 0x02;
     */
    private int type;
    public static int TYPE_ALERT = 0;
    public static int TYPE_HABIT = 1;
    public static int TYPE_REMINDER = 2;

Java
bean
public class FlashCardThemeBean implements Serializable {

    /**
     * 主题集合 最大值3个 Theme collection Maximum 3
     */
    public List<Theme> themes;

    /**
     * 最大支持数量
     * @return
     */
    public int getMaxNum() {
        return 3;
    }

    /**
     * 题目主题 Topic Theme
     */
    public static class Theme implements Serializable {
        /**
         * 主题ID：0 Vocabulary ，1 General Knowledge ，2 Flags
         * Topic IDs: 0 Vocabulary, 1 General Knowledge, 2 Flags
         */
        public int themeId;
        /**
         * 样式ID 1-5
         * Type IDs: 1-5
         */
        public int typeId;
        /**
         * 题目ID
         * Topic ID
         */
        public int topicId;
        /**
         * 题目标题
         * Topic Title
         */
        public String topicTitle;
        /**
         * 提示内容
         * Prompt Content
         */
        public String promptContent;
        /**
         * 题目内容
         * Topic Content
         */
        public List<BaseTopicType> topics;

        /**
         * 最大支持数量 Maximum supported number
         * @return
         */
        public int getMaxNum() {
            return 10;
        }
    }

    /**
     * 通用题目样式 Base topic style
     */
    public static class BaseTopicType implements Serializable {

    }

    /**
     * 题目样式一
     * 问题文字 + 问题答案（英，印地语）
     *
     * Question Type 1
     * Question Text + Answer (English, Hindi)
     */
    public static class TopicType1 extends BaseTopicType implements Serializable {
        /**
         * 问题文本-英语
         */
        public String questionTextByEN;
        /**
         * 问题答案-英语
         */
        public String answerTextByEN;
        /**
         * 问题文本-印地语
         */
        public String questionTextByHI;
        /**
         * 问题答案-印地语
         */
        public String answerTextByHI;
    }

    /**
     * 问题样式二
     * 问题图片 + 答案
     *
     * Question format 2:
     * Question image + answer
     */
    public static class TopicType2 extends BaseTopicType implements Serializable {
        /**
         * 问题图片 Problem Image
         */
        public Bitmap questionImg;
        /**
         * 答案图片 Answer Image
         */
        public Bitmap answerImg;
        /**
         * 问题答案 Answer Text
         */
        public String answerText;
    }

    /**
     * 问题样式三
     * 问题文字 + 问题图片 + 答案1，答案2，答案3，答案4
     *
     * Question format 3:
     * Question text + Question image + Answer 1, Answer 2, Answer 3, Answer 4
     */
    public static class TopicType3 extends BaseTopicType implements Serializable {
        /**
         * 问题文本 Question Text
         */
        public String questionText;
        /**
         * 问题图片 Question Image
         */
        public Bitmap questionImg;
        /**
         * 问题答案1 Answer to Question 1
         */
        public String answerText1;
        /**
         * 问题答案2 Answer to Question 2
         */
        public String answerText2;
        /**
         * 问题答案3 Answer to Question 3
         */
        public String answerText3;
        /**
         * 问题答案4 Answer to Question 4
         */
        public String answerText4;
    }

    /**
     * 问题样式四
     * 答案序号（1-4）+ 问题图片
     *
     * Question Type 4
     * Answer Number (1-4) + Question Image
     */
    public static class TopicType4 extends BaseTopicType implements Serializable {
        /**
         * 问题图片 Question Image
         */
        public Bitmap questionImg;
        /**
         * 答案序号1-4 Answer Number (1-4)
         */
        public int answerNumber;
    }

    /**
     * 问题样式五
     * 问题文字 + 答案序号（1-4）+ 答案1，答案2，答案3，答案4
     *
     * Question format 5:
     * Question text + answer number (1-4) + answer 1, answer 2, answer 3, answer 4
     */
    public static class TopicType5 extends BaseTopicType implements Serializable {
        /**
         * 问题文本 Question Text
         */
        public String questionText;
        /**
         * 答案序号1-4 Answer Number (1-4)
         */
        public int answerNumber;
        /**
         * 问题答案1 Answer to Question 1
         */
        public String answerText1;
        /**
         * 问题答案2 Answer to Question 2
         */
        public String answerText2;
        /**
         * 问题答案3 Answer to Question 3
         */
        public String answerText3;
        /**
         * 问题答案4 Answer to Question 4
         */
        public String answerText4;
    }
}FlashCardIdsBean

Java
bean
public class MedalInfoBean implements Serializable {

    /**
     * 勋章ID Medal ID
     *
     * @see com.zhapp.ble.callback.ChildrenCallBack.MedalId
     */
    private int medalId;
    /**
     * 已完成量，步数就是步数，挑战是已完成挑战次数....设备与App根据已完成量计算显示等级和进度
     * Completed quantity, steps are steps, challenges are completed challenge times... The device and App calculate the display level and progress based on the completed quantity
     */
    private int completedProgress;

CallBackUtils.berryBigFileResultCallBack = BerryBigFileResultCallBack { bean ->
}BerryBigFileResultCallBack
Java
callback
public interface BerryBigFileResultCallBack {
    /**
     * Berry协议大文件接收结果 Berry protocol large file reception results
     *
     * @param bean
     */
    void onBigFileResult(BerryBigFileResultBean bean);

    /**
     * 文件类型 Big file type
     */
    public enum BigFileType {
        /**
         * OTA
         */
        OTA(0x04),
        /**
         * 表盘 Dial
         */
        WATCHFACE(0x05),
        /**
         * agps
         */
        AGPS(0x06),
        /**
         * 多运动数据 Fitness sport data
         */
        FITNESS_SPORT_DATA(0x07),
        /**
         * 维测LOG Maintenance Log
         */
        DIMENSION_LOG(0x08),
        /**
         * dump log
         */
        DUMP_LOG(0x09),
        /**
         * 埋点LOG Point Log
         */
        POINT_LOG(0x0A),
        /**
         * 扩展文件 Extended file
         */
        EXTENDED_FILE(0X0B),
        /**
         * 自定义 自定义
         */
        CUSTOM(0x0C),
        /**
         * sensor log
         */
        SENSOR_LOG(0x0D),
        /**
         * 相册表盘背景 Album watch face background
         */
        ALBUM_DIAL_BG(0x0E),
        /**
         * 日常数据（包含步数，卡路里，距离） Daily data (including steps, calories, distance)
         */
        FITNESS_DAILY_DATA(0X0F),
        /**
         * 家长信息 Parent information
         */
        PARENTS_INFO(0x11),
        /**
         * 闪存卡 Flash card
         */
        FLASH_CARD(0X12),
        /**
         * 离线地图 Offline map
         */
        OFFLINE_MAP(0X13),
        /**
         * 未知 Unknown
         */
        UN_KNOWN(-1);
    }

    public enum ErrorType{
        /**
         * 异常 Abnormal
         */
        ERROR(0x02),
        /**
         * 设备ID异常 Device ID abnormal
         */
        DEVICE_ID_ERROR(0x03),
        /**
         * 文件大小超出设备存储空间 File size exceeds device storage space
         */
        OVER_SPACE(0x04),
        /**
         * 重复 repeat
         */
        DUPLICATED(0x05),
        /**
         * 内存不足 Low storage
         */
        LOW_STORAGE(0x06),
        /**
         * 电量不足 Low battery
         */
        LOW_BATTERY(0x07),
        /**
         * 降级，强制升级？ Force upgrade?
         */
        DOWNGRADE(0x08),
        /**
         * 内存不足询问是否删除表盘（只针对表盘） Low storage to ask whether to delete watch face
         */
        LOW_STORAGE_TO_ASK_DELETE(0x09),
        /**
         * 家长信息id错误 Parent information id error
         */
        PATRIARCH_INFO_ID_ERROR(0xA),
        /**
         * 闪存卡数量异常 Flash card count error
         */
        FLASH_CARD_COUNT_ERROR(0xB),
        /**
         * 未知 Unknown
         */
        UN_KNOWN(-1);
    }
}3.7 其他设置

     * 左键功能定义的设置 Setting the left button function definition
     *
     * @param setType
     */

    /**
     * 左键功能定义的设置参数
     */
    public enum CustomizeSettingType {
        /**
         * 通话功能 Call function
         */
        CALL_FUNCATION(0x00),
        /**
         * 指南针 Compass
         */
        COMPASS(0x01),
        /**
         * 气压/海拔 Barometric pressure/altitude
         */
        PRESSURE(0x02),
        /**
         * 呼吸 Breathing
         */
        BREATHE(0x03),
        /**
         * 秒表 Stopwatch
         */
        STOPWATCH(0x04),
        /**
         * 倒计时 Countdown
         */
        COUNTDOWN(0x05),
        /**
         * 闹钟 Alarm
         */
        ALARM_CLOCK(0x06),
        /**
         * 世界时钟 World Clock
         */
        WORLD_CLOCK(0x07),
        /**
         * 手电筒 Flashlight
         */
        FLASHLIGHT(0x08);
    }

C++
bean
public class VaultInfoBean implements Serializable {
    /**
     * 卡片标识ID Card id
     */
    private String cardId;
    /**
     * 类别(1：付款；2：个人；3：预定) Category (1: Payment; 2: Personal; 3: Booking)
     */
    private int sort;
    /**
     * app序号 app index
     *
     * 序号 Serial Number
     *  付款 Payment：1：Paytm，2：GPay，3：BHIM，4：PhonePe；
     *  个人 Personal：5：PAN，6：RC，7：Driving License，8：Business Cards，9：Insurance，10：Generic codes
     *  预定 Booking：11：Bookmyshow，12：Mera Events，13：Zomato District，14：Airlines，15：Air india，16：Spicejet，17：Metro，18：Bus, 19:Rail
     */
    private int appNum;
    /**
     * 是否常亮(0为不常亮，1为常亮) Is it always on (0 means not always on, 1 means always on)
     */
    private int alwaysOn;
    /**
     * 删除天数（30，60，90） Delete days (30, 60, 90)
     */
    private int deleteDays;
    /**
     * 密码，不传/传空为没有密码 Password, no password or empty password
     */
    private String password;
    /**
     * Vault卡片详细数据list Vault card detailed data list
     */
    private List<String> vaultStringList;
}3.8.3 录音（berry）
发送录音指令
Java
method

C++
callback
public interface RecordingCallBack {
    /**
     * 录音状态 Recording status
     *
     * @param bean
     */
    void onRecordingCmd(RecordingCmdBean bean);

    /**
     * 录音状态 Recording status
     */
    public enum RecordCmd {
        /**
         * 无意义 Meaningless
         */
        CMD_NONE(0x00),
        /**
         * 开始  Start
         */
        CMD_START(0x01),
        /**
         * 继续  Continue
         */
        CMD_CONTINUE(0x02),
        /**
         * 结束  Stop
         */
        CMD_STOP(0x03),
        /**
         * 暂停  Pause
         */
        CMD_PAUSE(0x04),
        /**
         * 可传输  Can be transferred
         */
        CMD_TRANSFER(0x05),
        /**
         * 网络异常 Network error
         */
        CMD_NETWORK_ERROR(0x06),
        /**
         * 转录失败 Transcription failed
         */
        CMD_FAILED_ERROR(0x07);
    }

    /**
     * 录音数据回调 Recording data callback
     * @param data
     */
    void onRecordingData(byte[] data);
}RecordingCmdBean
Java
callback
public class RecordingCmdBean implements Serializable {
    /**
     * 录音指令 Recording instructions
     *
     * @see com.zhapp.ble.callback.RecordingCallBack.RecordCmd
     */
    private int cmd;
    /**
     * 录音名称(即文件夹名称，最大长度不超过64 byte) Recording name (i.e. folder name, maximum length does not exceed 64 bytes)
     */
    private String recordName;

C++
callback
public interface RingSportCallBack {
    /**
     * 戒指运动状态 Ring movement status
     *
     * @param bean
     */
    void onRingSportStatus(RingSportStatusBean bean);

    /**
     * 戒指运动数据 Ring motion data
     *
     * @param bean
     */
    void onRingSportData(RingSportDataBean bean);

    /**
     * 戒指运动状态 Ring movement status
     */
    public enum RingSportStatus {
        /**
         * 无运动 None
         */
        SPORT_STATUS_NONE(0x00),
        /**
         * 运动开始 Sport start
         */
        SPORT_STATUS_START(0x01),
        /**
         * 运动暂停 Sport pause
         */
        SPORT_STATUS_PAUSE(0x02),
        /**
         * 运动继续 Sport continue
         */
        SPORT_STATUS_RESUME(0x03),
        /**
         * 运动结束 Sport end
         */
        SPORT_STATUS_END(0x04);
    }

    public enum RingSportEndReason {
        /**
         * 正常结束 Normal end
         */
        SPORT_END_REASON_NONE(0x00),
        /**
         * 低电量 Low power
         */
        SPORT_END_REASON_LOW_POWER(0x01),
        /**
         * 8小时超时 Timeout
         */
        SPORT_END_REASON_TIMEOUT(0x02),
        /**
         * 内存不够 No memory
         */
        SPORT_END_REASON_NO_MEMORY(0x03),
        /**
         * 充电中 Charge
         */
        SPORT_END_REASON_CHARGE(0x04);
    }

    public enum RingSportStartResult {
        /**
         * 正常开始 Normal start
         */
        SPORT_START_RESULT_NONE(0x00),
        /**
         * 低电量 Low power
         */
        SPORT_START_RESULT_LOW_POWER(0x01),
        /**
         * 未佩戴 Not wear
         */
        SPORT_START_RESULT_UN_WEAR(0x02),
        /**
         * 充电 Charging
         */
        SPORT_START_RESULT_CHARGING(0x03),
        /**
         * 运动中 Sporting
         */
        SPORT_START_RESULT_SPORTING(0x04);
    }

}RingSportStatusBean
C++
bean
public class RingSportStatusBean implements Serializable {
    /**
     * false:  没有运动需要同步  true: 有运动需要进行同步
     * false: No movement needs to be synchronized true: There is movement that needs to be synchronized
     */
    public boolean isSportNoSync;
    /**
     * false: 没有运动进行         true: 有运动进行
     * false: No exercise is taking place true: Exercise is taking place
     */
    public boolean isSporting;
    /**
     * 本次运动结束的原因 The reason for the end of this movement
     *
     * @see com.zhapp.ble.callback.RingSportCallBack.RingSportEndReason
     */
    public int endReason;
    /**
     * 运动状态 Movement status
     *
     * @see com.zhapp.ble.callback.RingSportCallBack.RingSportStatus
     */
    public int sportStatus;
    /**
     * 开始运动状态 Start exercise status
     *
     * @see com.zhapp.ble.callback.RingSportCallBack.RingSportStartResult
     */
    public int startResult;
    /**
     * 运动类型 Sport type
     */
    public int sportType;
    /**
     * 开启运动时的时间戳 Start exercise timestamp
     */
    public long startTime;
    /**
     * 运动的持续时长 Duration of movement
     */
    public int duration;
}RingSportDataBean

C++
callback
public interface ActiveMeasureCallBack {
    /**
     * 设备发送主动测量 成功或失败回调 The device sends an active measurement success or failure callback
     *
     * @param statusBean
     */
    void onMeasureStatus(ActiveMeasureStatusBean statusBean);

    /**
     * 设备发送主动测量 测量中的数据回调 The device sends an active measurement in progress data callback
     *
     * @param measuringBean
     */
    void onMeasuring(ActiveMeasuringBean measuringBean);

    /**
     * 设备发送主动测量 测量结果数据回调 The device sends an active measurement result data callback
     *
     * @param resultBean
     */
    void onMeasureResult(ActiveMeasureResultBean resultBean);

    /**
     * 测量类型枚举 Measurement Type Enumeration
     */
    public enum MeasureType {
        /**
         * 心率 heart rate
         */
        HEART_RATE(0x01),
        /**
         * 血氧 blood oxygen
         */
        BLOOD_OXYGEN(0x02),
        /**
         * 压力 stress
         */
        STRESS_HRV(0x03),
        /**
         * 体温 body temperature
         */
        BODY_TEMPERATURE(0x04),
        /**
         * 体温 body temperature
         */
        STRESS(0x05);
    }

    /**
     * 测量类型枚举 Measurement Type Enumeration
     */
    public enum MeasureErrorReason {
        /**
         * 成功了的  succeeded
         */
        SUCCEEDED(0x00),
        /**
         * 未佩戴 not wearing
         */
        NOT_WRIST(0x01),
        /**
         * 数据异常 data error
         */
        DATA_ERROR(0x02);
    }
}ActiveMeasureParamsBean
Java
bean
public class ActiveMeasureParamsBean implements Serializable {
    /**
     * 发起测量的类型 Type of measurement initiated
     * @see com.zhapp.ble.callback.ActiveMeasureCallBack.MeasureType
     */
    private int measureType;

    /**
     * 是否测量 false: 结束测量  true:开始测量
     * Whether to measure false: end measurement true: start measurement
     */
    private boolean switchMeasure;
}ActiveMeasureStatusBean

bean

     * 秒级 整体测量需要的时间。30s或者60s不一定，用来做APP超时判断
     * Seconds: The time required for overall measurement. 30s or 60s is not necessarily the case, used for APP timeout judgment
     */
    private int measureTime;
}ActiveMeasuringBean

Java
callback
public interface RingSleepInvalidReasonCallBack {
    /**
     * 戒指睡眠无效原因 Reasons why ring sleep is ineffective
     *
     * @param reason
     */
    void onInvalidReason(RingSleepInvalidReasonBean reason);

    public enum RingSleepInvalidReason {
        /**
         * 心率升高 Increased heart rate
         */
        REASON_ELEVATE_HEART_RATE(0x01),
        /**
         * 活动计数过高和清醒比例较高 Activity count is high and wakefulness is high
         */
        REASON_ACTIVITY_AWAKE(0x02),
        /**
         * 1和2同时发生 1 and 2 occur at the same time
         */
        REASON_ONE_AND_TWO(0x03),
        /**
         * 睡眠时长较短 Sleep duration is short
         */
        REASON_SLEEP_DURATION_SHORT(0x04),
        /**
         * 1和4同时发生 1 and 4 occur at the same time
         */
        REASON_ONE_FOUR(0x05),
        /**
         * 2和4同时发生 2 and 4 occur at the same time
         */
        REASON_TWO_FOUR(0x06),
        /**
         * 1、2、4同时发生 1 and 2 and 4 occur at the same time
         */
        REASON_ONE_TWO_FOUR(0x07);
    }
}RingSleepInvalidReasonBean
Java
bean
public class RingSleepInvalidReasonBean implements Serializable {
    /**
     * 无效原因 Invalid reason
     * {@link RingSleepInvalidReason}
     */
    private int invalidReason;

通用
SendCmdState
Java
bean
public enum SendCmdState {
    /**
     * 成功 Succeed
     */
    SUCCEED,
    /**
     * 不支持的指令 Not supported command
     */
    NOT_SUPPORT,
    /**
     * 依赖项未就绪 Dependency not ready
     */
    DEPENDENCY_NOT_READY,
    /**
     * 失败 Failed
     */
    FAILED,
    /**
     * 参数错误 Parameter error
     */
    PARAM_ERROR,
    /**
     * 未知 Unknown
     */
    UNKNOWN,
    /**
     * 未初始化 Uninitialized （蓝牙未开启/蓝牙断开/ sdk release()  Bluetooth not enabled/Bluetooth disconnected/sdk release()）
     */
    UNINITIALIZED,
    /**
     * 超时 Timeout
     */
    TIMEOUT
}SettingTimeBean

public class CommonReminderBean implements Serializable {
    /**
     * 是否开启 Is it enabled?
     */
    public boolean isOn;
    /**
     * 开始时间 Start time
     */
    public SettingTimeBean startTime;
    /**
     * 结束时间 End time
     */
    public SettingTimeBean endTime;
    /**
     * 间隔时间 （分钟 限定输入的是60的倍数 Interval time (minutes) is limited to multiples of 60
     */
    public int frequency;
    /**
     * 午休免打扰  （吃药无此参数） Do not disturb during lunch break (this parameter is not available when taking medicine)
     */
    public boolean noDisturbInLaunch;

    /**
     * 午休开始时间 Lunch break start time
     */
    public SettingTimeBean startNoonTime;
    /**
     * 午休结束时间 Lunch break end time
     */
    public SettingTimeBean endNoonTime;

    /**
     * 提醒的单次喝水量 毫升 Drink water volume (milliliters)
     */
    public int drinkWater;
